---------- src/lib/auth-helpers.ts ----------
import { getServerSession } from "next-auth";
import { authOptions } from "@/lib/auth";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export class UnauthorizedError extends Error {
  constructor(message = "Not authenticated") {
    super(message);
    this.name = "UnauthorizedError";
  }
}

export class ForbiddenError extends Error {
  constructor(message = "Not authorized for this resource") {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ForbiddenError";
  }
}

export async function requireUser(): Promise<{ id: string; email: string }> {
  const session = await getServerSession(authOptions);
  const userId = (session?.user as { id?: string } | undefined)?.id;

  if (!session?.user || !userId) {
    throw new UnauthorizedError();
  }

  return {
    id: userId,
    email: session.user.email ?? "",
  };
}

export async function requireWorkspaceAccess(
  userId: string,
  workspaceId: string
): Promise<{ role: string }> {
  const membership = await prisma.workspaceMember.findUnique({
    where: {
      userId_workspaceId: {
        userId,
        workspaceId,
      },
    },
  });

  if (!membership) {
    throw new ForbiddenError("You do not have access to this workspace");
  }

  return { role: membership.role };
}

export async function requireWebsiteAccess(userId: string, websiteId: string) {
  const website = await prisma.website.findUnique({
    where: { id: websiteId },
    include: { business: true },
  });

  if (!website) {
    throw new ForbiddenError("Website not found or not accessible");
  }

  await requireWorkspaceAccess(userId, website.business.workspaceId);

  return website;
}

export function authErrorResponse(error: unknown): { status: number; body: { error: string } } {
  if (error instanceof UnauthorizedError) {
    return { status: 401, body: { error: "Authentication required" } };
  }
  if (error instanceof ForbiddenError) {
    return { status: 403, body: { error: "Forbidden" } };
  }
  return { status: 500, body: { error: "Server error" } };
}

export async function requireAnalysisAccess(userId: string, analysisId: string) {
  const analysis = await prisma.analysis.findUnique({
    where: { id: analysisId },
    include: { website: { include: { business: true } } },
  });

  if (!analysis) {
    throw new ForbiddenError("Analysis not found or not accessible");
  }

  await requireWorkspaceAccess(userId, analysis.website.business.workspaceId);

  return analysis;
}

---------- src/lib/auth.ts ----------
import type { NextAuthOptions } from "next-auth";
import CredentialsProvider from "next-auth/providers/credentials";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export const authOptions: NextAuthOptions = {
  session: {
    strategy: "jwt",
  },
  pages: {
    signIn: "/login",
  },
  providers: [
    CredentialsProvider({
      name: "Credentials",
      credentials: {
        email: { label: "Email", type: "email" },
        password: { label: "Password", type: "password" },
      },
      async authorize(credentials) {
        if (!credentials?.email || !credentials?.password) {
          return null;
        }

        const user = await prisma.user.findUnique({
          where: { email: credentials.email.toLowerCase().trim() },
        });

        if (!user) {
          return null;
        }

        const isValid = await bcrypt.compare(
          credentials.password,
          user.passwordHash
        );

        if (!isValid) {
          return null;
        }

        return {
          id: user.id,
          email: user.email,
          name: user.name ?? undefined,
        };
      },
    }),
  ],
  callbacks: {
    async jwt({ token, user }) {
      if (user) {
        token.userId = user.id;
      }
      return token;
    },
    async session({ session, token }) {
      if (session.user && token.userId) {
        (session.user as { id?: string }).id = token.userId as string;
      }
      return session;
    },
  },
};

---------- src/app/api/integrations/cloudflare/connect/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { upsertCloudflareConnection } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/connection-service";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, zoneId, apiToken } = body;

    if (!websiteId || !zoneId || !apiToken) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId, zoneId, and apiToken are required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const connection = await upsertCloudflareConnection(websiteId, zoneId, apiToken);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      connectionId: connection.id,
      zoneId: connection.zoneId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Cloudflare connect error:", error);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(error);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/cloudflare/sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { syncCloudflareTraffic } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/sync";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, days = 7 } = body;

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId is required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (
      typeof days !== "number" ||
      !Number.isInteger(days) ||
      days < 1 ||
      days > 90
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "days must be an integer between 1 and 90" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const until = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const sinceDate = new Date();
    sinceDate.setDate(sinceDate.getDate() - (days - 1));
    const since = sinceDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    const result = await syncCloudflareTraffic({ websiteId, since, until });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      connectionId: result.connectionId,
      requestedDays: result.requestedDays,
      syncedDays: result.syncedDays,
      skippedDays: result.skippedDays,
      availableFrom: result.availableFrom,
      availableTo: result.availableTo,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Cloudflare sync error:", error);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(error);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  verifyOAuthState,
  InvalidStateError,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  exchangeCodeForTokens,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  upsertGoogleAnalyticsConnection,
} from "@/lib/integrations/provider-connection-service";

function callbackUrl(
  req: NextRequest
): string {
  return (
    process.env
      .GOOGLE_GA4_REDIRECT_URI
      ?.trim() ||
    new URL(
      "/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback",
      req.url
    ).toString()
  );
}

function safeReturnTo(
  value:
    string | undefined
): string {
  if (
    value &&
    value.startsWith("/") &&
    !value.startsWith("//")
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return "/dashboard/audience?ga4=connected";
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const code =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("code");

    const state =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("state");

    const oauthError =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("error");

    if (oauthError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Google OAuth error: ${oauthError}`,
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !code ||
      !state
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Missing code or state.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    let statePayload;

    try {
      statePayload =
        verifyOAuthState(
          state
        );
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        InvalidStateError
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              error.message,
          },
          {
            status: 400,
          }
        );
      }

      throw error;
    }

    if (
      statePayload.userId !==
      user.id
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "State does not match authenticated user.",
        },
        {
          status: 403,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      statePayload.workspaceId
    );

    const tokens =
      await exchangeCodeForTokens(
        code,
        callbackUrl(req)
      );

    await upsertGoogleAnalyticsConnection(
      statePayload.workspaceId,
      tokens
    );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      new URL(
        safeReturnTo(
          statePayload.returnTo
        ),
        req.url
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[GA4 callback error]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google-analytics/connect/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  createOAuthState,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  buildGoogleAuthUrl,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  GA4_OAUTH_SCOPES,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/constants";

function callbackUrl(
  req: NextRequest
): string {
  return (
    process.env
      .GOOGLE_GA4_REDIRECT_URI
      ?.trim() ||
    new URL(
      "/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback",
      req.url
    ).toString()
  );
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const workspaceId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "workspaceId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "websiteId"
        )
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      workspaceId
    );

    if (websiteId) {
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );
    }

    const returnTo =
      websiteId
        ? `/dashboard/audience?websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}&ga4=connected`
        : "/dashboard/audience?ga4=connected";

    const state =
      createOAuthState(
        user.id,
        workspaceId,
        returnTo
      );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      buildGoogleAuthUrl(
        state,
        GA4_OAUTH_SCOPES,
        callbackUrl(req)
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google/callback/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  verifyOAuthState,
  InvalidStateError,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  exchangeCodeForTokens,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  upsertGoogleSearchConsoleConnection,
} from "@/lib/integrations/provider-connection-service";


function safeReturnTo(
  value:
    string | undefined
): string {
  if (
    value &&
    value.startsWith("/") &&
    !value.startsWith("//")
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return "/dashboard/audience?gsc=connected";
}


export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const code =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("code");

    const state =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("state");

    const oauthError =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("error");

    if (oauthError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Google OAuth error: ${oauthError}`,
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !code ||
      !state
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Missing code or state.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    let statePayload;

    try {
      statePayload =
        verifyOAuthState(
          state
        );
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        InvalidStateError
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              error.message,
          },
          {
            status: 400,
          }
        );
      }

      throw error;
    }

    if (
      statePayload.userId !==
      user.id
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "OAuth state does not match authenticated user.",
        },
        {
          status: 403,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      statePayload.workspaceId
    );

    const tokens =
      await exchangeCodeForTokens(
        code
      );

    await upsertGoogleSearchConsoleConnection(
      statePayload.workspaceId,
      tokens
    );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      new URL(
        safeReturnTo(
          statePayload.returnTo
        ),
        req.url
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[GSC callback]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown Google OAuth error"
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google/connect/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  createOAuthState,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  buildGoogleAuthUrl,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  GSC_OAUTH_SCOPES,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/constants";


export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    const requestedWorkspaceId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("workspaceId")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    let workspaceId:
      string | null =
        requestedWorkspaceId;

    if (websiteId) {
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

      const website =
        await prisma.website.findUnique({
          where: {
            id:
              websiteId,
          },

          select: {
            business: {
              select: {
                workspaceId:
                  true,
              },
            },
          },
        });

      if (!website) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "Website not found.",
          },
          {
            status: 404,
          }
        );
      }

      workspaceId =
        website.business
          .workspaceId;
    }

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId or workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (!websiteId) {
      await requireWorkspaceAccess(
        user.id,
        workspaceId
      );
    }

    const returnTo =
      websiteId
        ? `/dashboard/audience?websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}&gsc=connected`
        : "/dashboard/audience?gsc=connected";

    const state =
      createOAuthState(
        user.id,
        workspaceId,
        returnTo
      );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      buildGoogleAuthUrl(
        state,
        GSC_OAUTH_SCOPES
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

============================================================
5. OTHER GSC / GA4 / CLOUDFLARE / DATAFORSEO ROUTES

---------- src/app/api/traffic/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

const ALLOWED_DAYS = new Set([7, 15, 30]);

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");

    const days = Number(
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("days") ?? "7"
    );

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId is required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_DAYS.has(days)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "days must be 7, 15, or 30" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const connection =
      await prisma.cloudflareConnection.findUnique({
        where: { websiteId },
        select: {
          id: true,
          updatedAt: true,
        },
      });

    if (!connection) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        connected: false,
        source: "CLOUDFLARE",
        days,
        requestedDays: days,
        availableDays: 0,
        missingDays: days,
        availableFrom: null,
        availableTo: null,
        lastSyncedAt: null,
        snapshots: [],
      });
    }

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     * GET never contacts Cloudflare.
     *
     * Range buttons only read accumulated snapshots from PostgreSQL.
     * This makes 7 / 15 / 30 stable and avoids Cloudflare retention
     * failures when the user selects a longer period.
     */

    const todayUtc = new Date();

    const until = new Date(
      Date.UTC(
        todayUtc.getUTCFullYear(),
        todayUtc.getUTCMonth(),
        todayUtc.getUTCDate()
      )
    );

    // Only complete days.
    until.setUTCDate(
      until.getUTCDate() - 1
    );

    const since = new Date(until);

    since.setUTCDate(
      since.getUTCDate() - (days - 1)
    );

    const snapshots =
      await prisma.trafficSnapshot.findMany({
        where: {
          connectionId: connection.id,
          date: {
            gte: since,
            lte: until,
          },
        },
        orderBy: {
          date: "asc",
        },
      });

    const availableDays =
      snapshots.length;

    return NextResponse.json({
      connected: true,

      source: "CLOUDFLARE",

      days,

      requestedDays: days,

      availableDays,

      missingDays:
        Math.max(
          0,
          days - availableDays
        ),

      requestedRange: {
        start:
          since.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        end:
          until.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      },

      availableFrom:
        snapshots[0]?.date
          .toISOString()
          .slice(0, 10) ?? null,

      availableTo:
        snapshots[
          snapshots.length - 1
        ]?.date
          .toISOString()
          .slice(0, 10) ?? null,

      lastSyncedAt:
        connection.updatedAt.toISOString(),

      snapshots:
        snapshots.map((snapshot) => ({
          date:
            snapshot.date
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 10),

          totalRequests:
            snapshot.totalRequests,

          unclassifiedRequests:
            snapshot.unclassifiedRequests,

          verifiedBotRequests:
            snapshot.verifiedBotRequests,

          unverifiedBotRequests:
            snapshot.unverifiedBotRequests,

          threatsBlocked:
            snapshot.threatsBlocked,

          uniqueVisitors:
            snapshot.uniqueVisitors,

          bandwidthBytes:
            snapshot.bandwidthBytes.toString(),

          countryBreakdown:
            snapshot.countryBreakdown,

          deviceBreakdown:
            snapshot.deviceBreakdown,

          botCategoryBreakdown:
            snapshot.botCategoryBreakdown,
        })),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        { status }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[traffic GET]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not load stored traffic data.",
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/traffic/live/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getCloudflareConnectionWithToken,
} from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/connection-service";

const ENDPOINT =
  "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/graphql";

const MINUTE_QUERY = `
  query LiveMinutes(
    $zoneTag: string
    $start: Time
    $end: Time
  ) {
    viewer {
      zones(
        filter: {
          zoneTag: $zoneTag
        }
      ) {
        minutes: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 10000
          orderBy: [
            datetimeMinute_ASC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            datetimeMinute
          }

          sum {
            edgeResponseBytes
            visits
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

const DETAIL_QUERY = `
  query LiveDetail(
    $zoneTag: string
    $start: Time
    $end: Time
  ) {
    viewer {
      zones(
        filter: {
          zoneTag: $zoneTag
        }
      ) {
        totals: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 1
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          sum {
            visits
            edgeResponseBytes
          }
        }

        countries: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 100
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            clientCountryName
          }

          sum {
            visits
            edgeResponseBytes
          }
        }

        devices: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 30
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            clientDeviceType
          }
        }

        bots: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 100
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            verifiedBotCategory
          }
        }

        paths: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 20
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            clientRequestPath
          }
        }

        statuses: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 30
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            edgeResponseStatus
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

const REFERRER_QUERY = `
  query LiveReferrers(
    $zoneTag: string
    $start: Time
    $end: Time
  ) {
    viewer {
      zones(
        filter: {
          zoneTag: $zoneTag
        }
      ) {
        referrers: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 20
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            clientRefererHost
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

interface MinuteRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    datetimeMinute: string;
  };

  sum: {
    edgeResponseBytes: number;
    visits: number;
  };
}

interface DetailTotalRow {
  count: number;

  sum: {
    visits: number;
    edgeResponseBytes: number;
  };
}

interface CountryRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientCountryName: string;
  };

  sum: {
    visits: number;
    edgeResponseBytes: number;
  };
}

interface DeviceRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientDeviceType: string;
  };
}

interface BotRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    verifiedBotCategory: string;
  };
}

interface PathRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientRequestPath: string;
  };
}

interface StatusRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    edgeResponseStatus: number;
  };
}

interface ReferrerRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientRefererHost: string;
  };
}

interface MinuteData {
  viewer?: {
    zones?: Array<{
      minutes?: MinuteRow[];
    }>;
  };
}

interface DetailData {
  viewer?: {
    zones?: Array<{
      totals?: DetailTotalRow[];
      countries?: CountryRow[];
      devices?: DeviceRow[];
      bots?: BotRow[];
      paths?: PathRow[];
      statuses?: StatusRow[];
    }>;
  };
}

interface ReferrerData {
  viewer?: {
    zones?: Array<{
      referrers?: ReferrerRow[];
    }>;
  };
}

interface GraphQLResponse<T> {
  data?: T;

  errors?: Array<{
    message: string;
  }>;
}

interface Variables {
  zoneTag: string;
  start: string;
  end: string;
}

function n(
  value: unknown
): number {
  const parsed =
    Number(value);

  return Number.isFinite(parsed)
    ? parsed
    : 0;
}

function safeMessage(
  error: unknown
): string {
  return (
    error instanceof Error
      ? error.message
      : "Unknown Cloudflare error"
  )
    .replace(
      /Bearer\s+[^\s]+/gi,
      "Bearer [redacted]"
    )
    .slice(
      0,
      800
    );
}

async function runGraphQL<T>({
  token,
  query,
  variables,
}: {
  token: string;
  query: string;
  variables: Variables;
}): Promise<T> {
  const response =
    await fetch(
      ENDPOINT,
      {
        method: "POST",

        headers: {
          Authorization:
            `Bearer ${token}`,

          "Content-Type":
            "application/json",

          Accept:
            "application/json",
        },

        body:
          JSON.stringify({
            query,
            variables,
          }),

        cache:
          "no-store",
      }
    );

  const json: GraphQLResponse<T> =
    await response.json();

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      `Cloudflare GraphQL HTTP ${response.status}`
    );
  }

  if (
    json.errors &&
    json.errors.length > 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      json.errors
        .map(
          (error) =>
            error.message
        )
        .join("; ")
    );
  }

  if (!json.data) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cloudflare returned no GraphQL data."
    );
  }

  return json.data;
}

async function optionalGraphQL<T>(
  args: {
    token: string;
    query: string;
    variables: Variables;
  },
  label: string
): Promise<T | null> {
  try {
    return await runGraphQL<T>(
      args
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(
      `[traffic live ${label}]`,
      safeMessage(error)
    );

    return null;
  }
}

function countryName(
  value: string
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  if (
    value.length !== 2
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  try {
    const names =
      new Intl.DisplayNames(
        ["en"],
        {
          type: "region",
        }
      );

    return (
      names.of(
        value.toUpperCase()
      ) ?? value
    );
  } catch {
    return value;
  }
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId query parameter is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const connection =
      await getCloudflareConnectionWithToken(
        websiteId
      );

    if (!connection) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Cloudflare is not connected for this website.",
        },
        {
          status: 404,
        }
      );
    }

    const end =
      new Date();

    const start =
      new Date(
        end.getTime() -
          24 *
            60 *
            60 *
            1000
      );

    const variables: Variables = {
      zoneTag:
        connection.zoneId,

      start:
        start.toISOString(),

      end:
        end.toISOString(),
    };

    const minuteData =
      await runGraphQL<MinuteData>({
        token:
          connection.apiToken,

        query:
          MINUTE_QUERY,

        variables,
      });

    const [
      detailData,
      referrerData,
    ] =
      await Promise.all([
        optionalGraphQL<DetailData>(
          {
            token:
              connection.apiToken,

            query:
              DETAIL_QUERY,

            variables,
          },
          "detail"
        ),

        optionalGraphQL<ReferrerData>(
          {
            token:
              connection.apiToken,

            query:
              REFERRER_QUERY,

            variables,
          },
          "referrers"
        ),
      ]);

    const minuteRows =
      minuteData.viewer
        ?.zones?.[0]
        ?.minutes ??
      [];

    const detail =
      detailData?.viewer
        ?.zones?.[0];

    const timeline =
      minuteRows.map(
        (row) => ({
          time:
            row.dimensions
              .datetimeMinute,

          requests:
            n(
              row.count
            ),

          bandwidthBytes:
            n(
              row.sum
                ?.edgeResponseBytes
            ),

          uniques:
            n(
              row.sum
                ?.visits
            ),
        })
      );

    const requests24h =
      timeline.reduce(
        (
          total,
          row
        ) =>
          total +
          row.requests,
        0
      );

    const bandwidth24h =
      timeline.reduce(
        (
          total,
          row
        ) =>
          total +
          row.bandwidthBytes,
        0
      );

    const now =
      end.getTime();

    const requests15m =
      timeline
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            new Date(
              row.time
            ).getTime() >=
            now -
              15 *
                60 *
                1000
        )
        .reduce(
          (
            total,
            row
          ) =>
            total +
            row.requests,
          0
        );

    const requests60m =
      timeline
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            new Date(
              row.time
            ).getTime() >=
            now -
              60 *
                60 *
                1000
        )
        .reduce(
          (
            total,
            row
          ) =>
            total +
            row.requests,
          0
        );

    const totalDetail =
      detail
        ?.totals?.[0];

    const visits24h =
      n(
        totalDetail
          ?.sum?.visits
      );

    const countries =
      (
        detail
          ?.countries ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientCountryName
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            country:
              countryName(
                row.dimensions
                  .clientCountryName
              ),

            countryCode:
              row.dimensions
                .clientCountryName,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),

            visits:
              n(
                row.sum
                  ?.visits
              ),

            bandwidthBytes:
              n(
                row.sum
                  ?.edgeResponseBytes
              ),
          })
        );

    const devices =
      (
        detail
          ?.devices ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientDeviceType
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            device:
              row.dimensions
                .clientDeviceType,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const verifiedBots =
      (
        detail
          ?.bots ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .verifiedBotCategory
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            category:
              row.dimensions
                .verifiedBotCategory,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const verifiedBotRequests =
      verifiedBots.reduce(
        (
          total,
          row
        ) =>
          total +
          row.requests,
        0
      );

    const paths =
      (
        detail
          ?.paths ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientRequestPath
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            path:
              row.dimensions
                .clientRequestPath,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const statuses =
      (
        detail
          ?.statuses ??
        []
      )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            status:
              n(
                row.dimensions
                  .edgeResponseStatus
              ),

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            row.status > 0
        );

    const referrers =
      (
        referrerData
          ?.viewer
          ?.zones?.[0]
          ?.referrers ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientRefererHost
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            referrer:
              row.dimensions
                .clientRefererHost,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const hourlyMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          time: string;
          requests: number;
          visits: number;
          bandwidthBytes: number;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const row of
      timeline
    ) {
      const date =
        new Date(
          row.time
        );

      if (
        Number.isNaN(
          date.getTime()
        )
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      date.setUTCMinutes(
        0,
        0,
        0
      );

      const key =
        date.toISOString();

      const current =
        hourlyMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          time:
            key,

          requests:
            0,

          visits:
            0,

          bandwidthBytes:
            0,
        };

      current.requests +=
        row.requests;

      current.visits +=
        row.uniques;

      current.bandwidthBytes +=
        row.bandwidthBytes;

      hourlyMap.set(
        key,
        current
      );
    }

    const hourly =
      Array.from(
        hourlyMap.values()
      ).sort(
        (a, b) =>
          a.time.localeCompare(
            b.time
          )
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        connected:
          true,

        source:
          "CLOUDFLARE",

        mode:
          "NEAR_REAL_TIME",

        resolution:
          "1_MINUTE",

        refreshedAt:
          end.toISOString(),

        range: {
          start:
            start.toISOString(),

          end:
            end.toISOString(),
        },

        summary: {
          requests24h,
          requests60m,
          requests15m,
          visits24h,
          bandwidth24h,
          verifiedBotRequests,
        },

        timeline:
          timeline.map(
            (row) => ({
              time:
                row.time,

              requests:
                row.requests,

              bandwidthBytes:
                row.bandwidthBytes,
            })
          ),

        hourly,
        countries,
        devices,
        verifiedBots,
        paths,
        referrers,
        statuses,

        detailAvailable:
          detailData !==
          null,
      },
      {
        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store, max-age=0",
        },
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    const message =
      safeMessage(
        error
      );

    console.error(
      "[traffic live]",
      message
    );

    const permissionError =
      message.includes(
        "does not have access to the path"
      ) ||
      message.includes(
        "not authorized"
      ) ||
      message.includes(
        "Unauthorized"
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          permissionError
            ? "Cloudflare Analytics permission is required for live traffic."
            : `Live Cloudflare query failed: ${message}`,

        code:
          permissionError
            ? "CLOUDFLARE_ANALYTICS_PERMISSION_REQUIRED"
            : "CLOUDFLARE_LIVE_ERROR",
      },
      {
        status:
          permissionError
            ? 403
            : 502,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/traffic/sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { syncCloudflareTraffic } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/sync";

/*
 * Sync the latest 30 complete UTC days from Cloudflare.
 *
 * Each day is persisted as a TrafficSnapshot and the unique
 * connectionId/date constraint makes repeated syncs safe.
 *
 * The Audience 7 / 15 / 30 day views continue reading from the
 * persisted snapshots; this sync backfills the full monthly range.
 */
const CLOUDFLARE_SYNC_WINDOW_DAYS = 30;

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      (await req.json()) as {
        websiteId?: string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const connection =
      await prisma.cloudflareConnection.findUnique({
        where: { websiteId },
        select: {
          id: true,
        },
      });

    if (!connection) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Cloudflare is not connected for this website.",
        },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    const now = new Date();

    const until = new Date(
      Date.UTC(
        now.getUTCFullYear(),
        now.getUTCMonth(),
        now.getUTCDate()
      )
    );

    // Sync only complete days.
    until.setUTCDate(
      until.getUTCDate() - 1
    );

    const since = new Date(until);

    since.setUTCDate(
      since.getUTCDate() -
        (
          CLOUDFLARE_SYNC_WINDOW_DAYS -
          1
        )
    );

    const result =
      await syncCloudflareTraffic({
        websiteId,

        since:
          since.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        until:
          until.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      });

    /*
     * Touch connection.updatedAt so UI has a truthful sync timestamp.
     */
    await prisma.cloudflareConnection.update({
      where: {
        websiteId,
      },
      data: {
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,

      source: "CLOUDFLARE",

      syncWindowDays:
        CLOUDFLARE_SYNC_WINDOW_DAYS,

      requestedRange: {
        start:
          since.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        end:
          until.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      },

      ...result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        { status }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[traffic sync]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Cloudflare sync failed.",
      },
      { status: 502 }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/articles/[id]/analytics/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  authErrorResponse,
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getContentArticleById,
} from "@/lib/content/repository";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

const DAY_MS =
  24 *
  60 *
  60 *
  1000;

function startOfUtcDay(
  input:
    Date
) {
  const value =
    new Date(
      input
    );

  value.setUTCHours(
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0
  );

  return value;
}

function dateKey(
  input:
    Date
) {
  return input
    .toISOString()
    .slice(
      0,
      10
    );
}

function round(
  value:
    number,
  digits =
    1
) {
  const factor =
    10 **
    digits;

  return (
    Math.round(
      value *
        factor
    ) /
    factor
  );
}

function numberOrNull(
  value:
    unknown
) {
  if (
    value ===
      null ||
    value ===
      undefined
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const result =
    Number(
      value
    );

  return Number.isFinite(
    result
  )
    ? result
    : null;
}

function urlVariants(
  raw:
    string | null
) {
  if (!raw) {
    return [];
  }

  try {
    const parsed =
      new URL(
        raw
      );

    parsed.hash =
      "";

    const exact =
      parsed.toString();

    const withoutSlash =
      exact.endsWith(
        "/"
      )
        ? exact.slice(
            0,
            -1
          )
        : exact;

    const withSlash =
      withoutSlash +
      "/";

    return Array.from(
      new Set([
        exact,
        withoutSlash,
        withSlash,
      ])
    );
  } catch {
    return [
      raw,
    ];
  }
}

function countPhrase(
  text:
    string,
  phrase:
    string
) {
  const clean =
    phrase
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (!clean) {
    return 0;
  }

  const haystack =
    text.toLowerCase();

  let count =
    0;

  let from =
    0;

  while (true) {
    const index =
      haystack.indexOf(
        clean,
        from
      );

    if (
      index <
      0
    ) {
      break;
    }

    count +=
      1;

    from =
      index +
      clean.length;
  }

  return count;
}

function addJsonNumbers(
  value:
    unknown,
  totals:
    Map<
      string,
      number
    >,
  prefix =
    ""
) {
  if (
    value ===
      null ||
    value ===
      undefined
  ) {
    return;
  }

  if (
    typeof value ===
    "number" &&
    prefix
  ) {
    totals.set(
      prefix,
      (
        totals.get(
          prefix
        ) ??
        0
      ) +
        value
    );

    return;
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      value
    )
  ) {
    value.forEach(
      (
        item,
        index
      ) => {
        addJsonNumbers(
          item,
          totals,
          prefix
            ? `${prefix}.${index}`
            : String(
                index
              )
        );
      }
    );

    return;
  }

  if (
    typeof value ===
    "object"
  ) {
    for (
      const [
        key,
        child,
      ] of Object.entries(
        value as
          Record<
            string,
            unknown
          >
      )
    ) {
      addJsonNumbers(
        child,
        totals,
        prefix
          ? `${prefix}.${key}`
          : key
      );
    }
  }
}

function botGroup(
  label:
    string
) {
  const value =
    label.toLowerCase();

  if (
    /(^|[^a-z])(ai|llm)([^a-z]|$)|openai|gpt|anthropic|claude|perplexity|generative/.test(
      value
    )
  ) {
    return "AI";
  }

  if (
    /search|googlebot|bingbot|yandex|baidu/.test(
      value
    )
  ) {
    return "SEARCH";
  }

  return "OTHER";
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id: string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const article =
      await getContentArticleById(
        id
      );

    if (!article) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      article.brief
        .websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            article.brief
              .websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          business: {
            select: {
              workspaceId:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!website) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    const requestedDays =
      Number(
        req.nextUrl
          .searchParams
          .get(
            "days"
          ) ??
          "28"
      );

    const days =
      requestedDays ===
        7 ||
      requestedDays ===
        28 ||
      requestedDays ===
        90
        ? requestedDays
        : 28;

    const today =
      startOfUtcDay(
        new Date()
      );

    const currentStart =
      new Date(
        today.getTime() -
          (
            days -
            1
          ) *
            DAY_MS
      );

    const previousEnd =
      new Date(
        currentStart.getTime() -
          DAY_MS
      );

    const previousStart =
      new Date(
        previousEnd.getTime() -
          (
            days -
            1
          ) *
            DAY_MS
      );

    const targetUrl =
      article.publishedUrl ??
      null;

    const variants =
      urlVariants(
        targetUrl
      );

    const snapshots =
      variants.length
        ? await prisma.keywordSnapshot.findMany({
            where: {
              date: {
                gte:
                  previousStart,
              },

              pageUrl: {
                in:
                  variants,
              },

              keyword: {
                workspaceId:
                  website.business
                    .workspaceId,
              },
            },

            select: {
              date:
                true,

              impressions:
                true,

              clicks:
                true,

              ctr:
                true,

              position:
                true,

              pageUrl:
                true,

              keyword: {
                select: {
                  text:
                    true,
                },
              },
            },

            orderBy: {
              date:
                "asc",
            },

            take:
              10000,
          })
        : [];

    const currentRows =
      snapshots.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
          currentStart
      );

    const previousRows =
      snapshots.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
            previousStart &&
          row.date <=
            previousEnd
      );

    function summarizeSearch(
      rows:
        typeof snapshots
    ) {
      let impressions =
        0;

      let clicks =
        0;

      let weightedPosition =
        0;

      let positionWeight =
        0;

      for (
        const row
        of rows
      ) {
        const rowImpressions =
          row.impressions ??
          0;

        impressions +=
          rowImpressions;

        clicks +=
          row.clicks ??
          0;

        const position =
          numberOrNull(
            row.position
          );

        if (
          position !==
          null
        ) {
          const weight =
            rowImpressions >
            0
              ? rowImpressions
              : 1;

          weightedPosition +=
            position *
            weight;

          positionWeight +=
            weight;
        }
      }

      return {
        impressions,
        clicks,

        ctr:
          impressions >
          0
            ? round(
                (
                  clicks /
                  impressions
                ) *
                  100,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgPosition:
          positionWeight >
          0
            ? round(
                weightedPosition /
                  positionWeight,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgImpressionsPerDay:
          round(
            impressions /
              days,
            1
          ),

        avgClicksPerDay:
          round(
            clicks /
              days,
            2
          ),
      };
    }

    const currentSummary =
      summarizeSearch(
        currentRows
      );

    const previousSummary =
      summarizeSearch(
        previousRows
      );

    const dailyMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          date: string;
          impressions: number;
          clicks: number;
          weightedPosition: number;
          positionWeight: number;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const row
      of currentRows
    ) {
      const key =
        dateKey(
          row.date
        );

      const bucket =
        dailyMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          date:
            key,

          impressions:
            0,

          clicks:
            0,

          weightedPosition:
            0,

          positionWeight:
            0,
        };

      const impressions =
        row.impressions ??
        0;

      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      bucket.clicks +=
        row.clicks ??
        0;

      const position =
        numberOrNull(
          row.position
        );

      if (
        position !==
        null
      ) {
        const weight =
          impressions >
          0
            ? impressions
            : 1;

        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          position *
          weight;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          weight;
      }

      dailyMap.set(
        key,
        bucket
      );
    }

    const daily =
      Array.from(
        dailyMap.values()
      ).map(
        (
          bucket
        ) => ({
          date:
            bucket.date,

          impressions:
            bucket.impressions,

          clicks:
            bucket.clicks,

          avgPosition:
            bucket.positionWeight >
            0
              ? round(
                  bucket.weightedPosition /
                    bucket.positionWeight,
                  1
                )
              : null,
        })
      );

    const keywordMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          keyword: string;
          impressions: number;
          clicks: number;
          weightedPosition: number;
          positionWeight: number;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const row
      of currentRows
    ) {
      const key =
        row.keyword.text;

      const bucket =
        keywordMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          keyword:
            key,

          impressions:
            0,

          clicks:
            0,

          weightedPosition:
            0,

          positionWeight:
            0,
        };

      const impressions =
        row.impressions ??
        0;

      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      bucket.clicks +=
        row.clicks ??
        0;

      const position =
        numberOrNull(
          row.position
        );

      if (
        position !==
        null
      ) {
        const weight =
          impressions >
          0
            ? impressions
            : 1;

        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          position *
          weight;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          weight;
      }

      keywordMap.set(
        key,
        bucket
      );
    }

    const articleText =
      [
        article.title,
        article.h1,
        article.bodyMarkdown,
      ].join(
        "\n"
      );

    const keywords =
      Array.from(
        keywordMap.values()
      )
        .map(
          (
            bucket
          ) => {
            const avgPosition =
              bucket.positionWeight >
              0
                ? round(
                    bucket.weightedPosition /
                      bucket.positionWeight,
                    1
                  )
                : null;

            return {
              keyword:
                bucket.keyword,

              impressions:
                bucket.impressions,

              clicks:
                bucket.clicks,

              ctr:
                bucket.impressions >
                0
                  ? round(
                      (
                        bucket.clicks /
                        bucket.impressions
                      ) *
                        100,
                      1
                    )
                  : null,

              avgPosition,

              mentions:
                countPhrase(
                  articleText,
                  bucket.keyword
                ),
            };
          }
        )
        .sort(
          (
            a,
            b
          ) =>
            b.impressions -
              a.impressions ||
            b.clicks -
              a.clicks
        );

    const positioned =
      keywords.filter(
        (
          item
        ) =>
          item.avgPosition !==
          null
      );

    const search =
      {
        scope:
          targetUrl
            ? "ARTICLE_PAGE"
            : "NOT_PUBLISHED",

        targetUrl,

        days,

        hasData:
          currentRows.length >
          0,

        summary: {
          ...currentSummary,

          trackedQueries:
            keywords.length,

          top3:
            positioned.filter(
              (
                item
              ) =>
                (
                  item.avgPosition ??
                  Infinity
                ) <=
                3
            ).length,

          top10:
            positioned.filter(
              (
                item
              ) =>
                (
                  item.avgPosition ??
                  Infinity
                ) <=
                10
            ).length,

          top20:
            positioned.filter(
              (
                item
              ) =>
                (
                  item.avgPosition ??
                  Infinity
                ) <=
                20
            ).length,
        },

        previous:
          previousSummary,

        daily,

        keywords,
      };

    const trafficConnection =
      await prisma.cloudflareConnection.findUnique({
        where: {
          websiteId:
            article.brief
              .websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          snapshots: {
            where: {
              date: {
                gte:
                  previousStart,
              },
            },

            orderBy: {
              date:
                "asc",
            },

            select: {
              date:
                true,

              totalRequests:
                true,

              uniqueVisitors:
                true,

              verifiedBotRequests:
                true,

              unverifiedBotRequests:
                true,

              unclassifiedRequests:
                true,

              botCategoryBreakdown:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    const allTraffic =
      trafficConnection
        ?.snapshots ??
      [];

    const currentTraffic =
      allTraffic.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
          currentStart
      );

    const previousTraffic =
      allTraffic.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
            previousStart &&
          row.date <=
            previousEnd
      );

    function summarizeTraffic(
      rows:
        typeof allTraffic
    ) {
      const result =
        rows.reduce(
          (
            total,
            row
          ) => {
            total.totalRequests +=
              row.totalRequests;

            total.uniqueVisitors +=
              row.uniqueVisitors;

            total.verifiedBotRequests +=
              row.verifiedBotRequests;

            total.unverifiedBotRequests +=
              row.unverifiedBotRequests ??
              0;

            total.unclassifiedRequests +=
              row.unclassifiedRequests;

            return total;
          },
          {
            totalRequests:
              0,

            uniqueVisitors:
              0,

            verifiedBotRequests:
              0,

            unverifiedBotRequests:
              0,

            unclassifiedRequests:
              0,
          }
        );

      const observedDays =
        rows.length;

      return {
        ...result,

        observedDays,

        avgRequestsPerObservedDay:
          observedDays
            ? round(
                result.totalRequests /
                  observedDays,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgVisitorsPerObservedDay:
          observedDays
            ? round(
                result.uniqueVisitors /
                  observedDays,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgVerifiedBotsPerObservedDay:
          observedDays
            ? round(
                result.verifiedBotRequests /
                  observedDays,
                1
              )
            : null,
      };
    }

    const categoryTotals =
      new Map<
        string,
        number
      >();

    for (
      const row
      of currentTraffic
    ) {
      addJsonNumbers(
        row.botCategoryBreakdown,
        categoryTotals
      );
    }

    const categories =
      Array.from(
        categoryTotals.entries()
      )
        .map(
          ([
            label,
            requests,
          ]) => ({
            label,
            requests:
              round(
                requests,
                0
              ),
            group:
              botGroup(
                label
              ),
          })
        )
        .sort(
          (
            a,
            b
          ) =>
            b.requests -
            a.requests
        );

    const groupedBots =
      categories.reduce(
        (
          total,
          item
        ) => {
          if (
            item.group ===
            "AI"
          ) {
            total.ai +=
              item.requests;
          } else if (
            item.group ===
            "SEARCH"
          ) {
            total.search +=
              item.requests;
          } else {
            total.other +=
              item.requests;
          }

          return total;
        },
        {
          ai:
            0,

          search:
            0,

          other:
            0,
        }
      );

    const traffic =
      {
        scope:
          "SITE_WIDE",

        note:
          "Cloudflare traffic is currently stored for the whole website, not per article. These numbers are site context and are not attributed to this page.",

        hasData:
          currentTraffic.length >
          0,

        days,

        summary:
          summarizeTraffic(
            currentTraffic
          ),

        previous:
          summarizeTraffic(
            previousTraffic
          ),

        botGroups:
          groupedBots,

        categories,

        daily:
          currentTraffic.map(
            (
              row
            ) => ({
              date:
                dateKey(
                  row.date
                ),

              totalRequests:
                row.totalRequests,

              uniqueVisitors:
                row.uniqueVisitors,

              verifiedBotRequests:
                row.verifiedBotRequests,

              unclassifiedRequests:
                row.unclassifiedRequests,
            })
          ),
      };

    const briefLink =
      await prisma.contentBrief.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            article.briefId,
        },

        select: {
          outcomeActionId:
            true,
        },
      });

    const observations =
      briefLink
        ?.outcomeActionId
        ? await prisma.keywordOutcomeObservation.findMany({
            where: {
              actionId:
                briefLink.outcomeActionId,
            },

            orderBy: {
              observedAt:
                "asc",
            },

            select: {
              checkpoint:
                true,

              source:
                true,

              liveRank:
                true,

              liveRankState:
                true,

              impressions:
                true,

              clicks:
                true,

              ctr:
                true,

              averagePosition:
                true,

              pageUrl:
                true,

              observedAt:
                true,
            },
          })
        : [];

          // PLANNED_KEYWORDS_V1
      const plannedBrief =
        await prisma.contentBrief.findUnique({
          where: {
            id:
              article.briefId,
          },

          select: {
            primaryKeyword:
              true,

            secondaryKeywords:
              true,
          },
        });

      const plannedKeywords = {
        primary:
          plannedBrief
            ?.primaryKeyword ??
          "",

        secondary:
          plannedBrief
            ?.secondaryKeywords ??
          [],
      };


return NextResponse.json({
      search,

      plannedKeywords,

      traffic,

      outcome: {
        hasData:
          observations.length >
          0,

        observations:
          observations.map(
            (
              observation
            ) => ({
              checkpoint:
                observation.checkpoint,

              source:
                observation.source,

              liveRank:
                observation.liveRank,

              liveRankState:
                observation.liveRankState,

              impressions:
                observation.impressions,

              clicks:
                observation.clicks,

              ctr:
                numberOrNull(
                  observation.ctr
                ),

              averagePosition:
                numberOrNull(
                  observation.averagePosition
                ),

              pageUrl:
                observation.pageUrl,

              observedAt:
                observation.observedAt.toISOString(),
            })
          ),
      },
    });
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "content article analytics error",
      error
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  verifyOAuthState,
  InvalidStateError,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  exchangeCodeForTokens,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  upsertGoogleAnalyticsConnection,
} from "@/lib/integrations/provider-connection-service";

function callbackUrl(
  req: NextRequest
): string {
  return (
    process.env
      .GOOGLE_GA4_REDIRECT_URI
      ?.trim() ||
    new URL(
      "/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback",
      req.url
    ).toString()
  );
}

function safeReturnTo(
  value:
    string | undefined
): string {
  if (
    value &&
    value.startsWith("/") &&
    !value.startsWith("//")
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return "/dashboard/audience?ga4=connected";
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const code =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("code");

    const state =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("state");

    const oauthError =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("error");

    if (oauthError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Google OAuth error: ${oauthError}`,
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !code ||
      !state
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Missing code or state.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    let statePayload;

    try {
      statePayload =
        verifyOAuthState(
          state
        );
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        InvalidStateError
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              error.message,
          },
          {
            status: 400,
          }
        );
      }

      throw error;
    }

    if (
      statePayload.userId !==
      user.id
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "State does not match authenticated user.",
        },
        {
          status: 403,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      statePayload.workspaceId
    );

    const tokens =
      await exchangeCodeForTokens(
        code,
        callbackUrl(req)
      );

    await upsertGoogleAnalyticsConnection(
      statePayload.workspaceId,
      tokens
    );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      new URL(
        safeReturnTo(
          statePayload.returnTo
        ),
        req.url
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[GA4 callback error]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google-analytics/connect/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  createOAuthState,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  buildGoogleAuthUrl,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  GA4_OAUTH_SCOPES,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/constants";

function callbackUrl(
  req: NextRequest
): string {
  return (
    process.env
      .GOOGLE_GA4_REDIRECT_URI
      ?.trim() ||
    new URL(
      "/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback",
      req.url
    ).toString()
  );
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const workspaceId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "workspaceId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "websiteId"
        )
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      workspaceId
    );

    if (websiteId) {
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );
    }

    const returnTo =
      websiteId
        ? `/dashboard/audience?websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}&ga4=connected`
        : "/dashboard/audience?ga4=connected";

    const state =
      createOAuthState(
        user.id,
        workspaceId,
        returnTo
      );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      buildGoogleAuthUrl(
        state,
        GA4_OAUTH_SCOPES,
        callbackUrl(req)
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}
