---------- src/app/api/integrations/google/connect/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  createOAuthState,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  buildGoogleAuthUrl,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  GSC_OAUTH_SCOPES,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/constants";


export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    const requestedWorkspaceId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("workspaceId")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    let workspaceId:
      string | null =
        requestedWorkspaceId;

    if (websiteId) {
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

      const website =
        await prisma.website.findUnique({
          where: {
            id:
              websiteId,
          },

          select: {
            business: {
              select: {
                workspaceId:
                  true,
              },
            },
          },
        });

      if (!website) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "Website not found.",
          },
          {
            status: 404,
          }
        );
      }

      workspaceId =
        website.business
          .workspaceId;
    }

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId or workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (!websiteId) {
      await requireWorkspaceAccess(
        user.id,
        workspaceId
      );
    }

    const returnTo =
      websiteId
        ? `/dashboard/audience?websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}&gsc=connected`
        : "/dashboard/audience?gsc=connected";

    const state =
      createOAuthState(
        user.id,
        workspaceId,
        returnTo
      );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      buildGoogleAuthUrl(
        state,
        GSC_OAUTH_SCOPES
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/cloudflare/sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { syncCloudflareTraffic } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/sync";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, days = 7 } = body;

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId is required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (
      typeof days !== "number" ||
      !Number.isInteger(days) ||
      days < 1 ||
      days > 90
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "days must be an integer between 1 and 90" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const until = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const sinceDate = new Date();
    sinceDate.setDate(sinceDate.getDate() - (days - 1));
    const since = sinceDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    const result = await syncCloudflareTraffic({ websiteId, since, until });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      connectionId: result.connectionId,
      requestedDays: result.requestedDays,
      syncedDays: result.syncedDays,
      skippedDays: result.skippedDays,
      availableFrom: result.availableFrom,
      availableTo: result.availableTo,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Cloudflare sync error:", error);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(error);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/cloudflare/connect/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { upsertCloudflareConnection } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/connection-service";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, zoneId, apiToken } = body;

    if (!websiteId || !zoneId || !apiToken) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId, zoneId, and apiToken are required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const connection = await upsertCloudflareConnection(websiteId, zoneId, apiToken);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      connectionId: connection.id,
      zoneId: connection.zoneId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Cloudflare connect error:", error);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(error);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/audience/ga4/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getGa4Context,
  getGa4AccessToken,
  listAccessibleGa4Properties,
  verifyGa4PropertyAccess,
  saveGa4Property,
  getGa4Intelligence,
} from "@/lib/audience/ga4-intelligence";

const ALLOWED_DAYS =
  new Set([
    1,
    7,
    15,
    30,
  ]);

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim();

    const days =
      Number(
        req.nextUrl.searchParams
          .get("days") ??
        "30"
      );

    const mode =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("mode") ??
      "history";

    const country =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("country")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !ALLOWED_DAYS.has(
        days
      )
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "days must be 1, 7, 15, or 30.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const context =
      await getGa4Context(
        websiteId
      );

    const token =
      await getGa4AccessToken(
        context.workspaceId
      );

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        connected:
          false,

        source:
          "GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_4",

        connectUrl:
          `/api/integrations/google-analytics/connect?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(
            context.workspaceId
          )}&websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}`,
      });
    }

    if (
      !context.ga4PropertyId
    ) {
      const properties =
        await listAccessibleGa4Properties(
          token
        );

      return NextResponse.json({
        connected:
          true,

        source:
          "GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_4",

        needsProperty:
          true,

        properties,
      });
    }

    const report =
      await getGa4Intelligence({
        propertyId:
          context.ga4PropertyId,

        token,

        days,

        mode,

        country,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      connected:
        true,

      source:
        "GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_4",

      needsProperty:
        false,

      ...report,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    const message =
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error";

    console.error(
      "[GA4 intelligence]",
      message
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          `Google Analytics request failed: ${message}`,
      },
      {
        status: 502,
      }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?:
          string;

        propertyId?:
          string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId
        ?.trim();

    const propertyId =
      body.propertyId
        ?.trim()
        .replace(
          /^properties\//,
          ""
        );

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !propertyId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId and propertyId are required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const context =
      await getGa4Context(
        websiteId
      );

    const token =
      await getGa4AccessToken(
        context.workspaceId
      );

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Google Analytics is not connected.",
        },
        {
          status: 409,
        }
      );
    }

    await verifyGa4PropertyAccess(
      propertyId,
      token
    );

    await saveGa4Property(
      websiteId,
      propertyId
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      success:
        true,

      propertyId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "Could not save GA4 property.",
      },
      {
        status: 502,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/audience/search-queries/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import {
  gscProvider,
  ProviderNotConnectedError,
  SitePermissionError,
} from "@/lib/keywords/gsc-provider";
import type { KeywordProvenance } from "@/lib/keywords/types";

const ALLOWED_DAYS =
  new Set([7, 15, 30]);

const MAX_RESULTS = 50;

interface Bucket {
  query: string;
  clicks: number;
  impressions: number;
  weightedPosition: number;
  positionWeight: number;
  pages: Map<
    string,
    {
      page: string;
      clicks: number;
      impressions: number;
    }
  >;
}

function round(
  value: number,
  digits = 1
) {
  const factor = 10 ** digits;

  return (
    Math.round(value * factor) /
    factor
  );
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get(
        "websiteId"
      );

    const days = Number(
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get(
        "days"
      ) ?? "7"
    );

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_DAYS.has(days)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "days must be 7, 15, or 30",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: websiteId,
        },
        select: {
          url: true,
          business: {
            select: {
              workspaceId: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!website) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found",
        },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    const workspaceId =
      website.business.workspaceId;

    const connection =
      await gscProvider.getConnectionStatus(
        workspaceId
      );

    if (
      connection.status !==
      "CONNECTED"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        connected: false,
        source:
          "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",
        days,
        range: null,
        summary: null,
        queries: [],

        state:
          "DISCONNECTED",

        connectUrl:
          `/api/integrations/google/connect?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(
            workspaceId
          )}&websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}`,
      });
    }

    const now = new Date();

    const endDate = new Date(
      Date.UTC(
        now.getUTCFullYear(),
        now.getUTCMonth(),
        now.getUTCDate()
      )
    );

    endDate.setUTCDate(
      endDate.getUTCDate() - 1
    );

    const startDate =
      new Date(endDate);

    startDate.setUTCDate(
      startDate.getUTCDate() -
        (days - 1)
    );

    const performanceResult =
      await (async () => {
        try {
          const rows =
            await gscProvider.fetchSearchPerformance({
              workspaceId,
              siteUrl:
                website.url,
              startDate,
              endDate,
            });

          return {
            ok:
              true as const,
            rows,
          };
        } catch (error) {
          return {
            ok:
              false as const,
            error,
          };
        }
      })();

    if (
      !performanceResult.ok
    ) {
      const providerError =
        performanceResult.error;

      const connectUrl =
        `/api/integrations/google/connect?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(
          workspaceId
        )}&websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
          websiteId
        )}`;

      if (
        providerError instanceof
        SitePermissionError
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          connected:
            true,

          source:
            "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

          days,

          range: {
            start:
              startDate
                .toISOString()
                .slice(0, 10),

            end:
              endDate
                .toISOString()
                .slice(0, 10),
          },

          summary:
            null,

          queries:
            [],

          state:
            "PROPERTY_UNAVAILABLE",

          message:
            "The connected Google account does not have Search Console access to this website.",

          connectUrl,
        });
      }

      const providerMessage =
        providerError instanceof Error
          ? providerError.message
          : "";

      const reconnectRequired =
        providerError instanceof
          ProviderNotConnectedError ||
        /invalid_grant|invalid credentials|unauthorized|authentication|refresh token|token has been expired|token has been revoked/i.test(
          providerMessage
        );

      if (
        reconnectRequired
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          connected:
            false,

          source:
            "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

          days,

          range:
            null,

          summary:
            null,

          queries:
            [],

          state:
            "RECONNECT_REQUIRED",

          message:
            "Google Search Console authorization needs to be refreshed.",

          connectUrl,
        });
      }

      console.error(
        "[audience search queries provider]",
        providerMessage ||
          "Unknown Search Console provider error"
      );

      return NextResponse.json({
        connected:
          true,

        source:
          "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

        days,

        range: {
          start:
            startDate
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 10),

          end:
            endDate
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 10),
        },

        summary:
          null,

        queries:
          [],

        state:
          "TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE",

        message:
          "Search Console is temporarily unavailable.",

        connectUrl,
      });
    }

    const rawRows =
      performanceResult.rows;

    const provenance:
      KeywordProvenance = {
        provider:
          "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",
        retrievedAt: new Date(),
        dateRangeStart:
          startDate,
        dateRangeEnd:
          endDate,
        country: "us",
        device: "DESKTOP",
      };

    const normalized =
      gscProvider.normalizeRows(
        rawRows,
        provenance
      );

    const buckets =
      new Map<string, Bucket>();

    for (
      const {
        keyword,
        snapshot,
      } of normalized
    ) {
      const query =
        keyword.text.trim();

      if (!query) continue;

      const clicks =
        Math.max(
          0,
          Number(
            snapshot.clicks ?? 0
          )
        );

      const impressions =
        Math.max(
          0,
          Number(
            snapshot.impressions ?? 0
          )
        );

      const position =
        snapshot.position == null
          ? null
          : Number(
              snapshot.position
            );

      const page =
        snapshot.pageUrl?.trim() ??
        "";

      let bucket =
        buckets.get(query);

      if (!bucket) {
        bucket = {
          query,
          clicks: 0,
          impressions: 0,
          weightedPosition: 0,
          positionWeight: 0,
          pages: new Map(),
        };

        buckets.set(
          query,
          bucket
        );
      }

      bucket.clicks += clicks;
      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      if (
        position !== null &&
        Number.isFinite(position)
      ) {
        const weight =
          Math.max(
            1,
            impressions
          );

        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          position * weight;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          weight;
      }

      if (page) {
        const existing =
          bucket.pages.get(page) ?? {
            page,
            clicks: 0,
            impressions: 0,
          };

        existing.clicks +=
          clicks;

        existing.impressions +=
          impressions;

        bucket.pages.set(
          page,
          existing
        );
      }
    }

    const queries =
      [...buckets.values()]
        .map((bucket) => {
          const topPage =
            [...bucket.pages.values()]
              .sort(
                (a, b) =>
                  b.clicks -
                    a.clicks ||
                  b.impressions -
                    a.impressions
              )[0]?.page ?? null;

          const ctr =
            bucket.impressions > 0
              ? (
                  bucket.clicks /
                  bucket.impressions
                ) * 100
              : 0;

          const avgPosition =
            bucket.positionWeight > 0
              ? (
                  bucket.weightedPosition /
                  bucket.positionWeight
                )
              : null;

          return {
            query:
              bucket.query,

            clicks:
              bucket.clicks,

            impressions:
              bucket.impressions,

            ctr:
              round(ctr, 1),

            avgPosition:
              avgPosition === null
                ? null
                : round(
                    avgPosition,
                    1
                  ),

            topPage,
          };
        })
        .sort(
          (a, b) =>
            b.clicks -
              a.clicks ||
            b.impressions -
              a.impressions ||
            (a.avgPosition ??
              999) -
              (b.avgPosition ??
                999)
        );

    const clicks =
      queries.reduce(
        (sum, row) =>
          sum + row.clicks,
        0
      );

    const impressions =
      queries.reduce(
        (sum, row) =>
          sum +
          row.impressions,
        0
      );

    const weighted =
      [...buckets.values()]
        .reduce(
          (acc, bucket) => {
            acc.total +=
              bucket.weightedPosition;

            acc.weight +=
              bucket.positionWeight;

            return acc;
          },
          {
            total: 0,
            weight: 0,
          }
        );

    return NextResponse.json({
      connected: true,

      source:
        "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

      days,

      range: {
        start:
          startDate
            .toISOString()
            .slice(0, 10),

        end:
          endDate
            .toISOString()
            .slice(0, 10),
      },

      summary: {
        clicks,

        impressions,

        ctr:
          impressions > 0
            ? round(
                (
                  clicks /
                  impressions
                ) * 100,
                1
              )
            : 0,

        avgPosition:
          weighted.weight > 0
            ? round(
                weighted.total /
                  weighted.weight,
                1
              )
            : null,

        uniqueQueries:
          queries.length,
      },

      queries:
        queries.slice(
          0,
          MAX_RESULTS
        ),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        { status }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[audience/search-queries]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not load Google Search Console data.",
      },
      { status: 502 }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/market-breakdown/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import {
  getMarketBreakdown,
} from "@/lib/keywords/market-breakdown";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const workspaceId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");

    const keyword =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("keyword");

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "workspaceId is required." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    if (!keyword?.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "keyword is required." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const markets = await getMarketBreakdown(keyword.trim());

    return NextResponse.json({
      keyword: keyword.trim(),
      markets,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: err.message },
        { status: 502 },
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[keywords/market-breakdown error]",
      err,
    );

    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(err);

    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/volume-sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { updateKeywordVolumes } from "@/lib/keywords/volume-service";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { workspaceId, forceRefresh, market, language } = body as {
      workspaceId?: string;
      forceRefresh?: boolean;
      market?: string;
      language?: string;
    };

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const result = await updateKeywordVolumes(
      workspaceId,
      forceRefresh ?? false,
      market ?? "US",
      language ?? "English",
    );
    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("[keywords/volume-sync error]", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/explore/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import { getCachedRelatedKeywords, RateLimitError } from "@/lib/keywords/suggestion-cache";
import { fetchTrendingQueries } from "@/lib/keywords/trends-provider";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const workspaceId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");
    const seed = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("seed");
    const limitParam = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("limit");
    const limit = limitParam ? Math.min(50, Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(limitParam, 10) || 50)) : 50;

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!seed || !seed.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "seed is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const { suggestions, fromCache } = await getCachedRelatedKeywords(workspaceId, seed.trim(), limit);
    const sorted = [...suggestions].sort((a, b) => (b.searchVolume ?? 0) - (a.searchVolume ?? 0));

    // Best-effort and free -- never let a Trends failure break the response.
    const trending = await fetchTrendingQueries(seed.trim());

    return NextResponse.json({ seed: seed.trim(), suggestions: sorted, fromCache, trending });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 429 });
    }
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("[keywords/explore error]", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/overview/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { normalizeKeyword } from "@/lib/keywords/normalize";
import { fetchSearchVolume, fetchKeywordDifficulty } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";

const SOURCE = "DATAFORSEO_RELATED";
const VOLUME_SOURCE = "DATAFORSEO_VOLUME";
const CACHE_TTL_DAYS = 30;

const MARKETS = [
  { code: "US", label: "US", flag: "🇺🇸", language: "English", locationName: "United States" },
  { code: "CA", label: "CA", flag: "🇨🇦", language: "English", locationName: "Canada" },
  { code: "GB", label: "GB", flag: "🇬🇧", language: "English", locationName: "United Kingdom" },
  { code: "AU", label: "AU", flag: "🇦🇺", language: "English", locationName: "Australia" },
];

function isFresh(fetchedAt: Date): boolean {
  return Date.now() - fetchedAt.getTime() < CACHE_TTL_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}

interface MarketMetrics {
  volume: number | null;
  kd: number | null;
  cpc: number | null;
}

async function getMetricsForMarket(keywordText: string, market: string, language: string): Promise<MarketMetrics> {
  const normalized = normalizeKeyword(keywordText);
  const cacheKey = `${normalized}:${market}:${language}`;

  // Check volume cache first
  const cached = await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.findUnique({
    where: { normalized_source: { normalized: cacheKey, source: VOLUME_SOURCE } },
  });
  if (cached && isFresh(cached.fetchedAt)) {
    const p = cached.payload as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      volume: typeof p.volume === "number" ? p.volume : null,
      kd: typeof p.kd === "number" ? p.kd : null,
      cpc: typeof p.cpc === "number" ? p.cpc : null,
    };
  }

  // Also check related keywords cache
  const relatedCacheKey = `${normalized}:${market}:${language}`;
  const relatedCached = await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.findUnique({
    where: { normalized_source: { normalized: relatedCacheKey, source: SOURCE } },
  });
  if (relatedCached && isFresh(relatedCached.fetchedAt) && Array.isArray(relatedCached.payload)) {
    const item = (relatedCached.payload as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
      .find((p) => typeof p.keyword === "string" && p.keyword === keywordText);
    if (item) {
      return {
        volume: typeof item.searchVolume === "number" ? item.searchVolume : null,
        kd: typeof item.keywordDifficulty === "number" ? item.keywordDifficulty : null,
        cpc: typeof item.cpc === "number" ? item.cpc : null,
      };
    }
  }

  // Fetch directly from DataForSEO
  const marketObj = MARKETS.find(m => m.code === market);
  if (!marketObj) return { volume: null, kd: null, cpc: null };

  try {
    const [volumeResults, diffResults] = await Promise.all([
      fetchSearchVolume([keywordText], marketObj.locationName, language),
      fetchKeywordDifficulty([keywordText], marketObj.locationName, language),
    ]);
    const vr = volumeResults[0];
    const dr = diffResults[0];
    const metrics: MarketMetrics = {
      volume: vr?.searchVolume ?? null,
      kd: dr?.keywordDifficulty ?? null,
      cpc: vr?.cpc ?? null,
    };
    // Cache the result
    await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.upsert({
      where: { normalized_source: { normalized: cacheKey, source: VOLUME_SOURCE } },
      create: { normalized: cacheKey, source: VOLUME_SOURCE, payload: metrics as unknown as Prisma.InputJsonValue },
      update: { payload: metrics as unknown as Prisma.InputJsonValue, fetchedAt: new Date() },
    });
    return metrics;
  } catch {
    return { volume: null, kd: null, cpc: null };
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams;
    const workspaceId = sp.get("workspaceId");
    const keywordText = sp.get("keyword");
    const market = sp.get("market") ?? "US";
    const language = sp.get("language") ?? "English";

    if (!workspaceId) return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    if (!keywordText) return NextResponse.json({ error: "keyword is required." }, { status: 400 });

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    // Get metrics for selected market
    const [selectedMetrics, keyword] = await Promise.all([
      getMetricsForMarket(keywordText, market, language),
      prisma.keyword.findFirst({
        where: { workspaceId, text: keywordText },
        include: { marketMetrics: true },
      }),
    ]);

    // DB overrides cache if available
    const dbMetric = keyword?.marketMetrics.find(m => m.market === market && m.language === language);
    const metrics = {
      volume: dbMetric?.volume ?? selectedMetrics.volume,
      kd: dbMetric?.difficulty ?? selectedMetrics.kd,
      cpc: dbMetric?.cpc ? Number(dbMetric.cpc) : selectedMetrics.cpc,
      competitionIndex: dbMetric?.competitionIndex ?? null,
      intent: keyword?.intent ?? null,
    };

    // Monthly history — from US related cache (most complete)
    let monthlySearches: { year: number; month: number; searchVolume: number }[] = [];
    let trend: { yearly: number | null; quarterly: number | null; monthly: number | null } | null = null;

    const allRelated = await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.findMany({ where: { source: SOURCE } });
    for (const cached of allRelated) {
      if (!Array.isArray(cached.payload)) continue;
      const item = (cached.payload as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
        .find((p) => typeof p.keyword === "string" && p.keyword === keywordText);
      if (item) {
        monthlySearches = (item.monthlySearches as typeof monthlySearches) ?? [];
        trend = (item.trend as typeof trend) ?? null;
        break;
      }
    }

    // Global volumes for all markets — parallel
    const globalVolumes = await Promise.all(MARKETS.map(async (m) => {
      const dbM = keyword?.marketMetrics.find(mm => mm.market === m.code && mm.language === m.language);
      if (dbM?.volume) return { code: m.code, label: m.label, flag: m.flag, volume: dbM.volume };
      const cached = await getMetricsForMarket(keywordText, m.code, m.language);
      return { code: m.code, label: m.label, flag: m.flag, volume: cached.volume };
    }));

    return NextResponse.json({
      keyword: keywordText,
      monthlySearches: monthlySearches.sort((a, b) => a.year - b.year || a.month - b.month),
      trend,
      globalVolumes: globalVolumes.filter((g): g is { code: string; label: string; flag: string; volume: number } => g.volume !== null),
      metrics,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/competitors/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import { RateLimitError } from "@/lib/keywords/competitor-cache";
import { listCompetitors } from "@/lib/competitors/repository";
import { analyzeCompetitor } from "@/lib/competitors/service";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const websiteId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");
    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const competitors = await listCompetitors(websiteId);
    return NextResponse.json({ competitors });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("competitors GET error", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, domain, name } = body as { websiteId?: string; domain?: string; name?: string };

    if (!websiteId || !domain || !domain.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId and domain are required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const website = await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const { competitor, snapshot } = await analyzeCompetitor({
      workspaceId: website.business.workspaceId,
      websiteId,
      domain,
      name,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ competitor, snapshot }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 429 });
    }
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("competitors POST error", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/competitors/[id]/refresh/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import { RateLimitError } from "@/lib/keywords/competitor-cache";
import { getCompetitor } from "@/lib/competitors/repository";
import { analyzeCompetitor } from "@/lib/competitors/service";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const { id } = await params;

    const competitor = await getCompetitor(id);
    if (!competitor) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Competitor not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, competitor.websiteId);

    const { snapshot } = await analyzeCompetitor({
      workspaceId: competitor.website.business.workspaceId,
      websiteId: competitor.websiteId,
      domain: competitor.domain,
      name: competitor.name ?? undefined,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ snapshot });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 429 });
    }
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("competitors/refresh POST error", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

7. PRISMA MODELS CONTAINING TOKENS / KEYS / SECRETS

model ProviderConnection {
  id              String         @id @default(cuid())
  workspaceId     String
  provider        ProviderType
  status          ProviderStatus @default(DISCONNECTED)
  accessTokenRef  String?
  refreshTokenRef String?
  expiresAt       DateTime?
  createdAt       DateTime       @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime       @updatedAt

9. ENCRYPTION IMPLEMENTATION
============================================================

---------- src/lib/integrations/token-crypto.ts ----------
import crypto from "crypto";

const ALGORITHM = "aes-256-gcm";
const IV_LENGTH_BYTES = 12;
const KEY_LENGTH_BYTES = 32;

function getEncryptionKey(): Buffer {
  const key = process.env.TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY;
  if (!key) {
    throw new Error("TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY is not set");
  }
  const buf = Buffer.from(key, "base64");
  if (buf.length !== KEY_LENGTH_BYTES) {
    throw new Error(
      `TOKEN_ENCRYPTION_KEY must decode to ${KEY_LENGTH_BYTES} bytes (base64-encoded AES-256 key)`
    );
  }
  return buf;
}

/**
 * Encrypts a plaintext token for storage.
 * Format: base64(iv).base64(authTag).base64(ciphertext)
 */
export function encryptToken(plaintext: string): string {
  const key = getEncryptionKey();
  const iv = crypto.randomBytes(IV_LENGTH_BYTES);
  const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv(ALGORITHM, key, iv);
  const ciphertext = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(plaintext, "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
  const authTag = cipher.getAuthTag();
  return [iv.toString("base64"), authTag.toString("base64"), ciphertext.toString("base64")].join(".");
}

/**
 * Decrypts a token previously produced by encryptToken.
 * Never log or return this value to a client.
 */
export function decryptToken(stored: string): string {
  const key = getEncryptionKey();
  const parts = stored.split(".");
  if (parts.length !== 3) {
    throw new Error("Malformed encrypted token");
  }
  const [ivB64, authTagB64, ciphertextB64] = parts;
  const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv(ALGORITHM, key, Buffer.from(ivB64, "base64"));
  decipher.setAuthTag(Buffer.from(authTagB64, "base64"));
  const plaintext = Buffer.concat([
    decipher.update(Buffer.from(ciphertextB64, "base64")),
    decipher.final(),
  ]);
  return plaintext.toString("utf8");
}

---------- src/app/api/traffic/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

const ALLOWED_DAYS = new Set([7, 15, 30]);

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");

    const days = Number(
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("days") ?? "7"
    );

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId is required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_DAYS.has(days)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "days must be 7, 15, or 30" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const connection =
      await prisma.cloudflareConnection.findUnique({
        where: { websiteId },
        select: {
          id: true,
          updatedAt: true,
        },
      });

    if (!connection) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        connected: false,
        source: "CLOUDFLARE",
        days,
        requestedDays: days,
        availableDays: 0,
        missingDays: days,
        availableFrom: null,
        availableTo: null,
        lastSyncedAt: null,
        snapshots: [],
      });
    }

    /*
     * IMPORTANT:
     * GET never contacts Cloudflare.
     *
     * Range buttons only read accumulated snapshots from PostgreSQL.
     * This makes 7 / 15 / 30 stable and avoids Cloudflare retention
     * failures when the user selects a longer period.
     */

    const todayUtc = new Date();

    const until = new Date(
      Date.UTC(
        todayUtc.getUTCFullYear(),
        todayUtc.getUTCMonth(),
        todayUtc.getUTCDate()
      )
    );

    // Only complete days.
    until.setUTCDate(
      until.getUTCDate() - 1
    );

    const since = new Date(until);

    since.setUTCDate(
      since.getUTCDate() - (days - 1)
    );

    const snapshots =
      await prisma.trafficSnapshot.findMany({
        where: {
          connectionId: connection.id,
          date: {
            gte: since,
            lte: until,
          },
        },
        orderBy: {
          date: "asc",
        },
      });

    const availableDays =
      snapshots.length;

    return NextResponse.json({
      connected: true,

      source: "CLOUDFLARE",

      days,

      requestedDays: days,

      availableDays,

      missingDays:
        Math.max(
          0,
          days - availableDays
        ),

      requestedRange: {
        start:
          since.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        end:
          until.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      },

      availableFrom:
        snapshots[0]?.date
          .toISOString()
          .slice(0, 10) ?? null,

      availableTo:
        snapshots[
          snapshots.length - 1
        ]?.date
          .toISOString()
          .slice(0, 10) ?? null,

      lastSyncedAt:
        connection.updatedAt.toISOString(),

      snapshots:
        snapshots.map((snapshot) => ({
          date:
            snapshot.date
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 10),

          totalRequests:
            snapshot.totalRequests,

          unclassifiedRequests:
            snapshot.unclassifiedRequests,

          verifiedBotRequests:
            snapshot.verifiedBotRequests,

          unverifiedBotRequests:
            snapshot.unverifiedBotRequests,

          threatsBlocked:
            snapshot.threatsBlocked,

          uniqueVisitors:
            snapshot.uniqueVisitors,

          bandwidthBytes:
            snapshot.bandwidthBytes.toString(),

          countryBreakdown:
            snapshot.countryBreakdown,

          deviceBreakdown:
            snapshot.deviceBreakdown,

          botCategoryBreakdown:
            snapshot.botCategoryBreakdown,
        })),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        { status }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[traffic GET]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not load stored traffic data.",
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/traffic/live/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,

const ENDPOINT =
  "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4/graphql";

const MINUTE_QUERY = `
  query LiveMinutes(
    $zoneTag: string
    $start: Time
    $end: Time
  ) {
    viewer {
      zones(
        filter: {
          zoneTag: $zoneTag
        }
      ) {
        minutes: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 10000
          orderBy: [
            datetimeMinute_ASC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            datetimeMinute
          }

          sum {
            edgeResponseBytes
            visits
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

const DETAIL_QUERY = `
  query LiveDetail(

            datetime_lt: $end

          }

            visits
            edgeResponseBytes
          }

            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end

          }

          count

            clientDeviceType
          }
        }

        bots: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 100
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            verifiedBotCategory
          }
        }

        paths: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 20
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            clientRequestPath
          }
        }

        statuses: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 30
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            edgeResponseStatus
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

const REFERRER_QUERY = `
  query LiveReferrers(
    $zoneTag: string
    $start: Time
    $end: Time
  ) {
    viewer {
      zones(
        filter: {
          zoneTag: $zoneTag
        }
      ) {
        referrers: httpRequestsAdaptiveGroups(
          limit: 20
          orderBy: [
            count_DESC
          ]
          filter: {
            datetime_geq: $start
            datetime_lt: $end
            requestSource: "eyeball"
          }
        ) {
          count

          dimensions {
            clientRefererHost
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
`;

interface MinuteRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    datetimeMinute: string;
  };

  sum: {
    edgeResponseBytes: number;
    visits: number;
  };
}

interface DetailTotalRow {
  count: number;

  sum: {
    visits: number;
    edgeResponseBytes: number;
  };
}

interface CountryRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientCountryName: string;
  };

  sum: {
    visits: number;
    edgeResponseBytes: number;
  };
}

interface DeviceRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientDeviceType: string;
  };
}

interface BotRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    verifiedBotCategory: string;
  };
}

interface PathRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientRequestPath: string;
  };
}

interface StatusRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    edgeResponseStatus: number;
  };
}

interface ReferrerRow {
  count: number;

  dimensions: {
    clientRefererHost: string;
  };
}

interface MinuteData {
  viewer?: {
    zones?: Array<{
      minutes?: MinuteRow[];
    }>;
  };
}

interface DetailData {
  viewer?: {
    zones?: Array<{
      totals?: DetailTotalRow[];
      countries?: CountryRow[];
      devices?: DeviceRow[];
      bots?: BotRow[];
      paths?: PathRow[];
      statuses?: StatusRow[];
    }>;
  };
}

interface ReferrerData {
  viewer?: {
    zones?: Array<{
      referrers?: ReferrerRow[];
    }>;
  };
}

interface GraphQLResponse<T> {
  data?: T;

  errors?: Array<{
    message: string;
  }>;
}

interface Variables {
  zoneTag: string;
  start: string;
  end: string;
}

function n(
  value: unknown
): number {
  const parsed =
    Number(value);

  return Number.isFinite(parsed)
    ? parsed
    : 0;
}

function safeMessage(
  error: unknown
): string {
  return (
    error instanceof Error
      ? error.message
      : "Unknown Cloudflare error"
  )
    .replace(
      /Bearer\s+[^\s]+/gi,
      "Bearer [redacted]"
    )
    .slice(
      0,
      800
    );
}

async function runGraphQL<T>({
  token,
  query,
  variables,
}: {
  token: string;
  query: string;
  variables: Variables;
}): Promise<T> {
  const response =
    await fetch(
      ENDPOINT,
      {
        method: "POST",

        headers: {
          Authorization:
            `Bearer ${token}`,

          "Content-Type":
            "application/json",

          Accept:
            "application/json",
        },

        body:
          JSON.stringify({
            query,
            variables,
          }),

        cache:
          "no-store",
      }
    );

  const json: GraphQLResponse<T> =
    await response.json();

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      `Cloudflare GraphQL HTTP ${response.status}`
    );
  }

  if (
    json.errors &&
    json.errors.length > 0
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      json.errors
        .map(
          (error) =>
            error.message
        )
        .join("; ")
    );
  }

  if (!json.data) {
    throw new Error(
      "Cloudflare returned no GraphQL data."
    );
  }

  return json.data;
}

async function optionalGraphQL<T>(
  args: {
    token: string;
    query: string;
    variables: Variables;
  },
  label: string
): Promise<T | null> {
  try {
    return await runGraphQL<T>(
      args
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn(
      `[traffic live ${label}]`,
      safeMessage(error)
    );

    return null;
  }
}

function countryName(
  value: string
): string {
  if (!value) {
    return "Unknown";
  }

  if (
    value.length !== 2
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  try {
    const names =
      new Intl.DisplayNames(
        ["en"],
        {
          type: "region",
        }
      );

    return (
      names.of(
        value.toUpperCase()
      ) ?? value
    );
  } catch {
    return value;
  }
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId query parameter is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const connection =
      await getCloudflareConnectionWithToken(
        websiteId
      );

    if (!connection) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Cloudflare is not connected for this website.",
        },
        {
          status: 404,
        }
      );
    }

    const end =
      new Date();

    const start =
      new Date(
        end.getTime() -
          24 *
            60 *
            60 *
            1000
      );

    const variables: Variables = {
      zoneTag:
        connection.zoneId,

      start:
        start.toISOString(),

      end:
        end.toISOString(),
    };

    const minuteData =
      await runGraphQL<MinuteData>({
        token:
          connection.apiToken,

        query:
          MINUTE_QUERY,

        variables,
      });

    const [
      detailData,
      referrerData,
    ] =
      await Promise.all([
        optionalGraphQL<DetailData>(
          {
            token:
              connection.apiToken,

            query:
              DETAIL_QUERY,

            variables,
          },
          "detail"
        ),

        optionalGraphQL<ReferrerData>(
          {
            token:
              connection.apiToken,

            query:
              REFERRER_QUERY,

            variables,
          },
          "referrers"
        ),
      ]);

    const minuteRows =
      minuteData.viewer
        ?.zones?.[0]
        ?.minutes ??
      [];

    const detail =
      detailData?.viewer
        ?.zones?.[0];

    const timeline =
      minuteRows.map(
        (row) => ({
          time:
            row.dimensions
              .datetimeMinute,

          requests:
            n(
              row.count
            ),

          bandwidthBytes:
            n(
              row.sum
                ?.edgeResponseBytes
            ),

          uniques:
            n(
              row.sum
                ?.visits
            ),
        })
      );

    const requests24h =
      timeline.reduce(
        (
          total,
          row
        ) =>
          total +
          row.requests,
        0
      );

    const bandwidth24h =
      timeline.reduce(
        (
          total,
          row
        ) =>
          total +
          row.bandwidthBytes,
        0
      );

    const now =
      end.getTime();

    const requests15m =
      timeline
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            new Date(
              row.time
            ).getTime() >=
            now -
              15 *
                60 *
                1000
        )
        .reduce(
          (
            total,
            row
          ) =>
            total +
            row.requests,
          0
        );

    const requests60m =
      timeline
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            new Date(
              row.time
            ).getTime() >=
            now -
              60 *
                60 *
                1000
        )
        .reduce(
          (
            total,
            row
          ) =>
            total +
            row.requests,
          0
        );

    const totalDetail =
      detail
        ?.totals?.[0];

    const visits24h =
      n(
        totalDetail
          ?.sum?.visits
      );

    const countries =
      (
        detail
          ?.countries ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientCountryName
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            country:
              countryName(
                row.dimensions
                  .clientCountryName
              ),

            countryCode:
              row.dimensions
                .clientCountryName,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),

            visits:
              n(
                row.sum
                  ?.visits
              ),

            bandwidthBytes:
              n(
                row.sum
                  ?.edgeResponseBytes
              ),
          })
        );

    const devices =
      (
        detail
          ?.devices ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientDeviceType
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            device:
              row.dimensions
                .clientDeviceType,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const verifiedBots =
      (
        detail
          ?.bots ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .verifiedBotCategory
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            category:
              row.dimensions
                .verifiedBotCategory,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const verifiedBotRequests =
      verifiedBots.reduce(
        (
          total,
          row
        ) =>
          total +
          row.requests,
        0
      );

    const paths =
      (
        detail
          ?.paths ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientRequestPath
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            path:
              row.dimensions
                .clientRequestPath,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const statuses =
      (
        detail
          ?.statuses ??
        []
      )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            status:
              n(
                row.dimensions
                  .edgeResponseStatus
              ),

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            row.status > 0
        );

    const referrers =
      (
        referrerData
          ?.viewer
          ?.zones?.[0]
          ?.referrers ??
        []
      )
        .filter(
          (row) =>
            Boolean(
              row.dimensions
                .clientRefererHost
            )
        )
        .map(
          (row) => ({
            referrer:
              row.dimensions
                .clientRefererHost,

            requests:
              n(
                row.count
              ),
          })
        );

    const hourlyMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          time: string;
          requests: number;
          visits: number;
          bandwidthBytes: number;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const row of
      timeline
    ) {
      const date =
        new Date(
          row.time
        );

      if (
        Number.isNaN(
          date.getTime()
        )
      ) {
        continue;
      }

      date.setUTCMinutes(
        0,
        0,
        0
      );

      const key =
        date.toISOString();

      const current =
        hourlyMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          time:
            key,

          requests:
            0,

          visits:
            0,

          bandwidthBytes:
            0,
        };

      current.requests +=
        row.requests;

      current.visits +=
        row.uniques;

      current.bandwidthBytes +=
        row.bandwidthBytes;

      hourlyMap.set(
        key,
        current
      );
    }

    const hourly =
      Array.from(
        hourlyMap.values()
      ).sort(
        (a, b) =>
          a.time.localeCompare(
            b.time
          )
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        connected:
          true,

        source:
          "CLOUDFLARE",

        mode:
          "NEAR_REAL_TIME",

        resolution:
          "1_MINUTE",

        refreshedAt:
          end.toISOString(),

        range: {
          start:
            start.toISOString(),

          end:
            end.toISOString(),
        },

        summary: {
          requests24h,
          requests60m,
          requests15m,
          visits24h,
          bandwidth24h,
          verifiedBotRequests,
        },

        timeline:
          timeline.map(
            (row) => ({
              time:
                row.time,

              requests:
                row.requests,

              bandwidthBytes:
                row.bandwidthBytes,
            })
          ),

        hourly,
        countries,
        devices,
        verifiedBots,
        paths,
        referrers,
        statuses,

        detailAvailable:
          detailData !==
          null,
      },
      {
        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store, max-age=0",
        },
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    const message =
      safeMessage(
        error
      );

    console.error(
      "[traffic live]",
      message
    );

    const permissionError =
      message.includes(
        "does not have access to the path"
      ) ||
      message.includes(
        "not authorized"
      ) ||
      message.includes(
        "Unauthorized"
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          permissionError
            ? "Cloudflare Analytics permission is required for live traffic."
            : `Live Cloudflare query failed: ${message}`,

        code:
          permissionError
            ? "CLOUDFLARE_ANALYTICS_PERMISSION_REQUIRED"
            : "CLOUDFLARE_LIVE_ERROR",
      },
      {
        status:
          permissionError
            ? 403
            : 502,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/traffic/sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { syncCloudflareTraffic } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/sync";

/*
 * Sync the latest 30 complete UTC days from Cloudflare.
 *
 * Each day is persisted as a TrafficSnapshot and the unique
 * connectionId/date constraint makes repeated syncs safe.
 *
 * The Audience 7 / 15 / 30 day views continue reading from the
 * persisted snapshots; this sync backfills the full monthly range.
 */
const CLOUDFLARE_SYNC_WINDOW_DAYS = 30;

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      (await req.json()) as {
        websiteId?: string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const connection =
      await prisma.cloudflareConnection.findUnique({
        where: { websiteId },
        select: {
          id: true,
        },
      });

    if (!connection) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Cloudflare is not connected for this website.",
        },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    const now = new Date();

    const until = new Date(
      Date.UTC(
        now.getUTCFullYear(),
        now.getUTCMonth(),
        now.getUTCDate()
      )
    );

    // Sync only complete days.
    until.setUTCDate(
      until.getUTCDate() - 1
    );

    const since = new Date(until);

    since.setUTCDate(
      since.getUTCDate() -
        (
          CLOUDFLARE_SYNC_WINDOW_DAYS -
          1
        )
    );

    const result =
      await syncCloudflareTraffic({
        websiteId,

        since:
          since.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        until:
          until.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      });

    /*
     * Touch connection.updatedAt so UI has a truthful sync timestamp.
     */
    await prisma.cloudflareConnection.update({
      where: {
        websiteId,
      },
      data: {
        updatedAt: new Date(),
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({
      ok: true,

      source: "CLOUDFLARE",

      syncWindowDays:
        CLOUDFLARE_SYNC_WINDOW_DAYS,

      requestedRange: {
        start:
          since.toISOString().slice(0, 10),

        end:
          until.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
      },

      ...result,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        { status }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[traffic sync]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Cloudflare sync failed.",
      },
      { status: 502 }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/auth/signup/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import bcrypt from "bcryptjs";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

const MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 8;

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const { email, password, name } = body as {
      email?: string;
      password?: string;
      name?: string;
    };

    if (!email || !password) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Email and password are required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(normalizedEmail)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Invalid email address." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (password.length < MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: `Password must be at least ${MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH} characters.` },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const existing = await prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { email: normalizedEmail },
    });

    if (existing) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Unable to create account." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 12);

    const result = await prisma.$transaction(async (tx) => {
      const user = await tx.user.create({
        data: {
          email: normalizedEmail,
          name: name?.trim() || null,
          passwordHash,
        },
      });

      const workspace = await tx.workspace.create({
        data: {
          name: name ? `${name}'s Workspace` : "My Workspace",
        },
      });

      await tx.workspaceMember.create({
        data: {
          userId: user.id,
          workspaceId: workspace.id,
          role: "OWNER",
        },
      });

      return { user, workspace };
    });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        user: { id: result.user.id, email: result.user.email },
        workspace: { id: result.workspace.id, name: result.workspace.name },
      },
      { status: 201 }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("signup error", err);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Server error." }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/internal/outcome-checkpoints/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  runOutcomeCheckpointEngine,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-checkpoint-engine";

export const runtime =
  "nodejs";

export const dynamic =
  "force-dynamic";

export const maxDuration =
  60;

export async function GET(
  request:
    NextRequest
) {
  const secret =
    process.env.CRON_SECRET
      ?.trim();

  if (
    !secret
  ) {
    console.error(
      "Outcome checkpoint cron is missing CRON_SECRET."
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Scheduler is not configured.",
      },
      {
        status:
          503,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  }

  const authorization =
    request.headers.get(
      "authorization"
    );

  if (
    authorization !==
    `Bearer ${secret}`
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Unauthorized.",
      },
      {
        status:
          401,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  }

  try {
    const result =
      await runOutcomeCheckpointEngine({
        limit:
          100,
      });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          true,

        result,
      },
      {
        status:
          200,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "Outcome checkpoint cron failed.",
      error
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Checkpoint run failed.",
      },
      {
        status:
          500,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/plan/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  buildFinalContentPlan,
} from "@/lib/content/final-content-plan";


export const dynamic =
  "force-dynamic";


export async function GET(
  req:
    NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "websiteId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const topic =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "topic"
        )
        ?.trim();

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !topic
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId and topic are required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          business: {
            select: {
              name:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!website) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found.",
        },
        {
          status: 404,
        }
      );
    }

    const plan =
      await buildFinalContentPlan({
        websiteId,
        topic,

        businessName:
          website.business
            .name,
      });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        plan,
      },
      {
        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store, max-age=0",
        },
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[content plan]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown content plan error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not build final content plan.",
      },
      {
        status: 500,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/articles/[id]/approve/route.ts ----------
import {
  ContentGateError,
} from "@/lib/content/content-gate";
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { getContentArticleById } from "@/lib/content/repository";
import { approveArticle } from "@/lib/content/service";
import {
  ArticlePublicationPolicyError,
} from "@/lib/content/article-publication-policy";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const user = await requireUser();

    const article = await getContentArticleById(id);
    if (!article) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Article not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, article.brief.websiteId);

    if (article.status !== "DRAFT" && article.status !== "IN_REVIEW") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: `Cannot approve an article with status ${article.status}.` }, { status: 409 });
    }

    const updated = await approveArticle(id);
    return NextResponse.json({ article: updated });
  } catch (err) {
    if (
      err instanceof
      ArticlePublicationPolicyError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article requires editorial or evidence review.",

          issues:
            err.issues,

          planWarnings:
            err.planWarnings,

          evidenceFindings:
            err.evidenceFindings,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }


    if (
      err instanceof
      ContentGateError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article blocked by Content Quality Gate.",

          issues:
            err.issues,

          claims:
            err.claimFindings,
        },
        {
          status: 409,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error("content/articles/[id]/approve POST error", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/articles/[id]/publish/route.ts ----------
import {
  ArticlePublicationPolicyError,
} from "@/lib/content/article-publication-policy";

import {
  ContentGateError,
} from "@/lib/content/content-gate";

import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getContentArticleById,
} from "@/lib/content/repository";

import {
  publishArticle,
} from "@/lib/content/service";

import {
  getCMSConnection,
} from "@/lib/cms/repository";

import {
  getProviderForType,
} from "@/lib/cms/registry";

import {
  assertOutcomeActionExecutable,
  markOutcomeActionExecuted,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-service";

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id:
          string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req
        .json()
        .catch(
          () => ({})
        ) as {
          outcomeActionId?:
            string;
        };

    const outcomeActionId =
      typeof body.outcomeActionId ===
        "string"
        ? body.outcomeActionId.trim()
        : "";

    const article =
      await getContentArticleById(
        id
      );

    if (
      !article
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        article.brief.websiteId
      );

    const effectiveOutcomeActionId =
      outcomeActionId ||
      article.brief.outcomeActionId?.trim() ||
      "";

    if (
      effectiveOutcomeActionId
    ) {
      try {
        await assertOutcomeActionExecutable({
          workspaceId:
            website.business.workspaceId,

          websiteId:
            article.brief.websiteId,

          actionId:
            effectiveOutcomeActionId,

          executionKind:
            "CONTENT_PUBLISH",

          primaryKeyword:
            article.brief.primaryKeyword,
        });
      } catch {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "This Growth OS action cannot be linked to this content execution.",
          },
          {
            status:
              409,
          }
        );
      }
    }

    const connection =
      await getCMSConnection(
        article.brief.websiteId
      );

    if (
      !connection
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "CMS is not connected for this website.",
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    const provider =
      getProviderForType(
        connection.cmsType
      );

    const updated =
      await publishArticle(
        provider,
        id
      );

    let outcomeLink:
      | {
          status:
            "EXECUTED";
          actionId:
            string;
        }
      | {
          status:
            "NOT_EXECUTED";
          actionId:
            string;
        }
      | {
          status:
            "FAILED_TO_RECORD";
          actionId:
            string;
        }
      | null =
      null;

    if (
      effectiveOutcomeActionId
    ) {
      if (
        updated.status ===
        "PUBLISHED"
      ) {
        try {
          await markOutcomeActionExecuted({
            workspaceId:
              website.business.workspaceId,

            websiteId:
              article.brief.websiteId,

            actionId:
              effectiveOutcomeActionId,

            executionKind:
              "CONTENT_PUBLISH",

            primaryKeyword:
              article.brief.primaryKeyword,

            contentArticleId:
              updated.id,
          });

          outcomeLink = {
            status:
              "EXECUTED",

            actionId:
              effectiveOutcomeActionId,
          };
        } catch (
          linkError
        ) {
          console.error(
            "content publish outcome link error",
            linkError
          );

          outcomeLink = {
            status:
              "FAILED_TO_RECORD",

            actionId:
              effectiveOutcomeActionId,
          };
        }
      } else {
        outcomeLink = {
          status:
            "NOT_EXECUTED",

          actionId:
            effectiveOutcomeActionId,
        };
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      outcomeLink
        ? {
            article:
              updated,

            outcomeLink,
          }
        : {
            article:
              updated,
          }
    );
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    if (
      err instanceof
      ArticlePublicationPolicyError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article requires editorial or evidence review.",

          issues:
            err.issues,

          planWarnings:
            err.planWarnings,

          evidenceFindings:
            err.evidenceFindings,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      err instanceof
      ContentGateError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article blocked by Content Quality Gate.",

          issues:
            err.issues,

          claims:
            err.claimFindings,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "content/articles/[id]/publish POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body:
        errBody,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      errBody,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}
