import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  authErrorResponse,
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getContentArticleById,
} from "@/lib/content/repository";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function GET(
  _req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id: string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const article =
      await getContentArticleById(
        id
      );

    if (!article) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      article.brief
        .websiteId
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      article,
    });
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

export async function PATCH(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id: string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const article =
      await getContentArticleById(
        id
      );

    if (!article) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      article.brief
        .websiteId
    );

    if (
      article.status ===
      "PUBLISHED"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Published articles cannot be edited here. Create a new revision instead.",
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      article.status ===
      "GENERATING"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "This article is still being generated.",
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    const body =
      await req.json();

    const data: {
      title?: string;
      metaDescription?: string;
      h1?: string;
      bodyMarkdown?: string;
      status?: "DRAFT";
    } = {};

    if (
      typeof body.title ===
      "string"
    ) {
      const value =
        body.title.trim();

      if (!value) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "Title cannot be empty.",
          },
          {
            status:
              400,
          }
        );
      }

      data.title =
        value;
    }

    if (
      typeof body.metaDescription ===
      "string"
    ) {
      data.metaDescription =
        body.metaDescription.trim();
    }

    if (
      typeof body.h1 ===
      "string"
    ) {
      const value =
        body.h1.trim();

      if (!value) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "H1 cannot be empty.",
          },
          {
            status:
              400,
          }
        );
      }

      data.h1 =
        value;
    }

    if (
      typeof body.bodyMarkdown ===
      "string"
    ) {
      data.bodyMarkdown =
        body.bodyMarkdown;
    }

    const hasChanges =
      Object.keys(
        data
      ).length >
      0;

    if (!hasChanges) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "No article changes were supplied.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    data.status =
      "DRAFT";

    const updated =
      await prisma.contentArticle.update({
        where: {
          id,
        },

        data,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      article:
        updated,
    });
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "content/articles/[id] PATCH error",
      error
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/articles/[id]/analytics/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  authErrorResponse,
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getContentArticleById,
} from "@/lib/content/repository";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

const DAY_MS =
  24 *
  60 *
  60 *
  1000;

function startOfUtcDay(
  input:
    Date
) {
  const value =
    new Date(
      input
    );

  value.setUTCHours(
    0,
    0,
    0,
    0
  );

  return value;
}

function dateKey(
  input:
    Date
) {
  return input
    .toISOString()
    .slice(
      0,
      10
    );
}

function round(
  value:
    number,
  digits =
    1
) {
  const factor =
    10 **
    digits;

  return (
    Math.round(
      value *
        factor
    ) /
    factor
  );
}

function numberOrNull(
  value:
    unknown
) {
  if (
    value ===
      null ||
    value ===
      undefined
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const result =
    Number(
      value
    );

  return Number.isFinite(
    result
  )
    ? result
    : null;
}

function urlVariants(
  raw:
    string | null
) {
  if (!raw) {
    return [];
  }

  try {
    const parsed =
      new URL(
        raw
      );

    parsed.hash =
      "";

    const exact =
      parsed.toString();

    const withoutSlash =
      exact.endsWith(
        "/"
      )
        ? exact.slice(
            0,
            -1
          )
        : exact;

    const withSlash =
      withoutSlash +
      "/";

    return Array.from(
      new Set([
        exact,
        withoutSlash,
        withSlash,
      ])
    );
  } catch {
    return [
      raw,
    ];
  }
}

function countPhrase(
  text:
    string,
  phrase:
    string
) {
  const clean =
    phrase
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (!clean) {
    return 0;
  }

  const haystack =
    text.toLowerCase();

  let count =
    0;

  let from =
    0;

  while (true) {
    const index =
      haystack.indexOf(
        clean,
        from
      );

    if (
      index <
      0
    ) {
      break;
    }

    count +=
      1;

    from =
      index +
      clean.length;
  }

  return count;
}

function addJsonNumbers(
  value:
    unknown,
  totals:
    Map<
      string,
      number
    >,
  prefix =
    ""
) {
  if (
    value ===
      null ||
    value ===
      undefined
  ) {
    return;
  }

  if (
    typeof value ===
    "number" &&
    prefix
  ) {
    totals.set(
      prefix,
      (
        totals.get(
          prefix
        ) ??
        0
      ) +
        value
    );

    return;
  }

  if (
    Array.isArray(
      value
    )
  ) {
    value.forEach(
      (
        item,
        index
      ) => {
        addJsonNumbers(
          item,
          totals,
          prefix
            ? `${prefix}.${index}`
            : String(
                index
              )
        );
      }
    );

    return;
  }

  if (
    typeof value ===
    "object"
  ) {
    for (
      const [
        key,
        child,
      ] of Object.entries(
        value as
          Record<
            string,
            unknown
          >
      )
    ) {
      addJsonNumbers(
        child,
        totals,
        prefix
          ? `${prefix}.${key}`
          : key
      );
    }
  }
}

function botGroup(
  label:
    string
) {
  const value =
    label.toLowerCase();

  if (
    /(^|[^a-z])(ai|llm)([^a-z]|$)|openai|gpt|anthropic|claude|perplexity|generative/.test(
      value
    )
  ) {
    return "AI";
  }

  if (
    /search|googlebot|bingbot|yandex|baidu/.test(
      value
    )
  ) {
    return "SEARCH";
  }

  return "OTHER";
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id: string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const article =
      await getContentArticleById(
        id
      );

    if (!article) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Article not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      article.brief
        .websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            article.brief
              .websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          business: {
            select: {
              workspaceId:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!website) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    const requestedDays =
      Number(
        req.nextUrl
          .searchParams
          .get(
            "days"
          ) ??
          "28"
      );

    const days =
      requestedDays ===
        7 ||
      requestedDays ===
        28 ||
      requestedDays ===
        90
        ? requestedDays
        : 28;

    const today =
      startOfUtcDay(
        new Date()
      );

    const currentStart =
      new Date(
        today.getTime() -
          (
            days -
            1
          ) *
            DAY_MS
      );

    const previousEnd =
      new Date(
        currentStart.getTime() -
          DAY_MS
      );

    const previousStart =
      new Date(
        previousEnd.getTime() -
          (
            days -
            1
          ) *
            DAY_MS
      );

    const targetUrl =
      article.publishedUrl ??
      null;

    const variants =
      urlVariants(
        targetUrl
      );

    const snapshots =
      variants.length
        ? await prisma.keywordSnapshot.findMany({
            where: {
              date: {
                gte:
                  previousStart,
              },

              pageUrl: {
                in:
                  variants,
              },

              keyword: {
                workspaceId:
                  website.business
                    .workspaceId,
              },
            },

            select: {
              date:
                true,

              impressions:
                true,

              clicks:
                true,

              ctr:
                true,

              position:
                true,

              pageUrl:
                true,

              keyword: {
                select: {
                  text:
                    true,
                },
              },
            },

            orderBy: {
              date:
                "asc",
            },

            take:
              10000,
          })
        : [];

    const currentRows =
      snapshots.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
          currentStart
      );

    const previousRows =
      snapshots.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
            previousStart &&
          row.date <=
            previousEnd
      );

    function summarizeSearch(
      rows:
        typeof snapshots
    ) {
      let impressions =
        0;

      let clicks =
        0;

      let weightedPosition =
        0;

      let positionWeight =
        0;

      for (
        const row
        of rows
      ) {
        const rowImpressions =
          row.impressions ??
          0;

        impressions +=
          rowImpressions;

        clicks +=
          row.clicks ??
          0;

        const position =
          numberOrNull(
            row.position
          );

        if (
          position !==
          null
        ) {
          const weight =
            rowImpressions >
            0
              ? rowImpressions
              : 1;

          weightedPosition +=
            position *
            weight;

          positionWeight +=
            weight;
        }
      }

      return {
        impressions,
        clicks,

        ctr:
          impressions >
          0
            ? round(
                (
                  clicks /
                  impressions
                ) *
                  100,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgPosition:
          positionWeight >
          0
            ? round(
                weightedPosition /
                  positionWeight,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgImpressionsPerDay:
          round(
            impressions /
              days,
            1
          ),

        avgClicksPerDay:
          round(
            clicks /
              days,
            2
          ),
      };
    }

    const currentSummary =
      summarizeSearch(
        currentRows
      );

    const previousSummary =
      summarizeSearch(
        previousRows
      );

    const dailyMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          date: string;
          impressions: number;
          clicks: number;
          weightedPosition: number;
          positionWeight: number;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const row
      of currentRows
    ) {
      const key =
        dateKey(
          row.date
        );

      const bucket =
        dailyMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          date:
            key,

          impressions:
            0,

          clicks:
            0,

          weightedPosition:
            0,

          positionWeight:
            0,
        };

      const impressions =
        row.impressions ??
        0;

      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      bucket.clicks +=
        row.clicks ??
        0;

      const position =
        numberOrNull(
          row.position
        );

      if (
        position !==
        null
      ) {
        const weight =
          impressions >
          0
            ? impressions
            : 1;

        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          position *
          weight;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          weight;
      }

      dailyMap.set(
        key,
        bucket
      );
    }

    const daily =
      Array.from(
        dailyMap.values()
      ).map(
        (
          bucket
        ) => ({
          date:
            bucket.date,

          impressions:
            bucket.impressions,

          clicks:
            bucket.clicks,

          avgPosition:
            bucket.positionWeight >
            0
              ? round(
                  bucket.weightedPosition /
                    bucket.positionWeight,
                  1
                )
              : null,
        })
      );

    const keywordMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          keyword: string;
          impressions: number;
          clicks: number;
          weightedPosition: number;
          positionWeight: number;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const row
      of currentRows
    ) {
      const key =
        row.keyword.text;

      const bucket =
        keywordMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          keyword:
            key,

          impressions:
            0,

          clicks:
            0,

          weightedPosition:
            0,

          positionWeight:
            0,
        };

      const impressions =
        row.impressions ??
        0;

      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      bucket.clicks +=
        row.clicks ??
        0;

      const position =
        numberOrNull(
          row.position
        );

      if (
        position !==
        null
      ) {
        const weight =
          impressions >
          0
            ? impressions
            : 1;

        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          position *
          weight;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          weight;
      }

      keywordMap.set(
        key,
        bucket
      );
    }

    const articleText =
      [
        article.title,
        article.h1,
        article.bodyMarkdown,
      ].join(
        "\n"
      );

    const keywords =
      Array.from(
        keywordMap.values()
      )
        .map(
          (
            bucket
          ) => {
            const avgPosition =
              bucket.positionWeight >
              0
                ? round(
                    bucket.weightedPosition /
                      bucket.positionWeight,
                    1
                  )
                : null;

            return {
              keyword:
                bucket.keyword,

              impressions:
                bucket.impressions,

              clicks:
                bucket.clicks,

              ctr:
                bucket.impressions >
                0
                  ? round(
                      (
                        bucket.clicks /
                        bucket.impressions
                      ) *
                        100,
                      1
                    )
                  : null,

              avgPosition,

              mentions:
                countPhrase(
                  articleText,
                  bucket.keyword
                ),
            };
          }
        )
        .sort(
          (
            a,
            b
          ) =>
            b.impressions -
              a.impressions ||
            b.clicks -
              a.clicks
        );

    const positioned =
      keywords.filter(
        (
          item
        ) =>
          item.avgPosition !==
          null
      );

    const search =
      {
        scope:
          targetUrl
            ? "ARTICLE_PAGE"
            : "NOT_PUBLISHED",

        targetUrl,

        days,

        hasData:
          currentRows.length >
          0,

        summary: {
          ...currentSummary,

          trackedQueries:
            keywords.length,

          top3:
            positioned.filter(
              (
                item
              ) =>
                (
                  item.avgPosition ??
                  Infinity
                ) <=
                3
            ).length,

          top10:
            positioned.filter(
              (
                item
              ) =>
                (
                  item.avgPosition ??
                  Infinity
                ) <=
                10
            ).length,

          top20:
            positioned.filter(
              (
                item
              ) =>
                (
                  item.avgPosition ??
                  Infinity
                ) <=
                20
            ).length,
        },

        previous:
          previousSummary,

        daily,

        keywords,
      };

    const trafficConnection =
      await prisma.cloudflareConnection.findUnique({
        where: {
          websiteId:
            article.brief
              .websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          snapshots: {
            where: {
              date: {
                gte:
                  previousStart,
              },
            },

            orderBy: {
              date:
                "asc",
            },

            select: {
              date:
                true,

              totalRequests:
                true,

              uniqueVisitors:
                true,

              verifiedBotRequests:
                true,

              unverifiedBotRequests:
                true,

              unclassifiedRequests:
                true,

              botCategoryBreakdown:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    const allTraffic =
      trafficConnection
        ?.snapshots ??
      [];

    const currentTraffic =
      allTraffic.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
          currentStart
      );

    const previousTraffic =
      allTraffic.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.date >=
            previousStart &&
          row.date <=
            previousEnd
      );

    function summarizeTraffic(
      rows:
        typeof allTraffic
    ) {
      const result =
        rows.reduce(
          (
            total,
            row
          ) => {
            total.totalRequests +=
              row.totalRequests;

            total.uniqueVisitors +=
              row.uniqueVisitors;

            total.verifiedBotRequests +=
              row.verifiedBotRequests;

            total.unverifiedBotRequests +=
              row.unverifiedBotRequests ??
              0;

            total.unclassifiedRequests +=
              row.unclassifiedRequests;

            return total;
          },
          {
            totalRequests:
              0,

            uniqueVisitors:
              0,

            verifiedBotRequests:
              0,

            unverifiedBotRequests:
              0,

            unclassifiedRequests:
              0,
          }
        );

      const observedDays =
        rows.length;

      return {
        ...result,

        observedDays,

        avgRequestsPerObservedDay:
          observedDays
            ? round(
                result.totalRequests /
                  observedDays,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgVisitorsPerObservedDay:
          observedDays
            ? round(
                result.uniqueVisitors /
                  observedDays,
                1
              )
            : null,

        avgVerifiedBotsPerObservedDay:
          observedDays
            ? round(
                result.verifiedBotRequests /
                  observedDays,
                1
              )
            : null,
      };
    }

    const categoryTotals =
      new Map<
        string,
        number
      >();

    for (
      const row
      of currentTraffic
    ) {
      addJsonNumbers(
        row.botCategoryBreakdown,
        categoryTotals
      );
    }

    const categories =
      Array.from(
        categoryTotals.entries()
      )
        .map(
          ([
            label,
            requests,
          ]) => ({
            label,
            requests:
              round(
                requests,
                0
              ),
            group:
              botGroup(
                label
              ),
          })
        )
        .sort(
          (
            a,
            b
          ) =>
            b.requests -
            a.requests
        );

    const groupedBots =
      categories.reduce(
        (
          total,
          item
        ) => {
          if (
            item.group ===
            "AI"
          ) {
            total.ai +=
              item.requests;
          } else if (
            item.group ===
            "SEARCH"
          ) {
            total.search +=
              item.requests;
          } else {
            total.other +=
              item.requests;
          }

          return total;
        },
        {
          ai:
            0,

          search:
            0,

          other:
            0,
        }
      );

    const traffic =
      {
        scope:
          "SITE_WIDE",

        note:
          "Cloudflare traffic is currently stored for the whole website, not per article. These numbers are site context and are not attributed to this page.",

        hasData:
          currentTraffic.length >
          0,

        days,

        summary:
          summarizeTraffic(
            currentTraffic
          ),

        previous:
          summarizeTraffic(
            previousTraffic
          ),

        botGroups:
          groupedBots,

        categories,

        daily:
          currentTraffic.map(
            (
              row
            ) => ({
              date:
                dateKey(
                  row.date
                ),

              totalRequests:
                row.totalRequests,

              uniqueVisitors:
                row.uniqueVisitors,

              verifiedBotRequests:
                row.verifiedBotRequests,

              unclassifiedRequests:
                row.unclassifiedRequests,
            })
          ),
      };

    const briefLink =
      await prisma.contentBrief.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            article.briefId,
        },

        select: {
          outcomeActionId:
            true,
        },
      });

    const observations =
      briefLink
        ?.outcomeActionId
        ? await prisma.keywordOutcomeObservation.findMany({
            where: {
              actionId:
                briefLink.outcomeActionId,
            },

            orderBy: {
              observedAt:
                "asc",
            },

            select: {
              checkpoint:
                true,

              source:
                true,

              liveRank:
                true,

              liveRankState:
                true,

              impressions:
                true,

              clicks:
                true,

              ctr:
                true,

              averagePosition:
                true,

              pageUrl:
                true,

              observedAt:
                true,
            },
          })
        : [];

          // PLANNED_KEYWORDS_V1
      const plannedBrief =
        await prisma.contentBrief.findUnique({
          where: {
            id:
              article.briefId,
          },

          select: {
            primaryKeyword:
              true,

            secondaryKeywords:
              true,
          },
        });

      const plannedKeywords = {
        primary:
          plannedBrief
            ?.primaryKeyword ??
          "",

        secondary:
          plannedBrief
            ?.secondaryKeywords ??
          [],
      };


return NextResponse.json({
      search,

      plannedKeywords,

      traffic,

      outcome: {
        hasData:
          observations.length >
          0,

        observations:
          observations.map(
            (
              observation
            ) => ({
              checkpoint:
                observation.checkpoint,

              source:
                observation.source,

              liveRank:
                observation.liveRank,

              liveRankState:
                observation.liveRankState,

              impressions:
                observation.impressions,

              clicks:
                observation.clicks,

              ctr:
                numberOrNull(
                  observation.ctr
                ),

              averagePosition:
                numberOrNull(
                  observation.averagePosition
                ),

              pageUrl:
                observation.pageUrl,

              observedAt:
                observation.observedAt.toISOString(),
            })
          ),
      },
    });
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "content article analytics error",
      error
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/serp/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  buildSerpIntelligence,
  SerpIntelligenceError,
} from "@/lib/content/serp-intelligence";


export const dynamic =
  "force-dynamic";


export async function GET(
  req:
    NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "websiteId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const keyword =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "keyword"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const locationName =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "locationName"
        )
        ?.trim() ||
      "United States";

    const languageName =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "languageName"
        )
        ?.trim() ||
      "English";

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !keyword
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId and keyword are required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const intelligence =
      await buildSerpIntelligence({
        keyword,
        locationName,
        languageName,
      });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        intelligence,
      },
      {
        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store, max-age=0",
        },
      }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      error instanceof
      SerpIntelligenceError
    ) {
      console.error(
        "[content SERP]",
        error.message
      );

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            error.message,
        },
        {
          status: 502,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[content SERP]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown SERP intelligence error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not build SERP intelligence.",
      },
      {
        status: 500,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/facts/discover/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  discoverBusinessFacts,
} from "@/lib/content/fact-discovery";


export async function POST(
  req:
    NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?:
          string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId
        ?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const result =
      await discoverBusinessFacts({
        websiteId,
        actorUserId:
          user.id,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      result,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(
      "[fact-discovery] route error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    if (
      process.env.NODE_ENV !==
        "production" &&
      status >= 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            err instanceof Error
              ? `${err.name}: ${err.message}`
              : "Unknown fact discovery error",
        },
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/facts/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import type {
  BusinessFactType,
} from "@prisma/client";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

const TYPES = new Set([
  "CAPABILITY",
  "METRIC",
  "DATA_SOURCE",
  "POLICY",
  "DIFFERENTIATOR",
  "COMPLIANCE",
  "OTHER",
]);

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    const items =
      await prisma.businessFact.findMany({
        where: {
          businessId:
            website.businessId,
        },
        include: {
          events: {
            orderBy: {
              createdAt:
                "desc",
            },
          },
        },
        orderBy: {
          createdAt:
            "desc",
        },
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      items,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } = authErrorResponse(err);

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?: string;
        type?: string;
        claim?: string;
        sourceUrl?: string;
        sourceLabel?: string;
        evidence?: string;
        confidence?: number;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim();

    const claim =
      body.claim?.trim();

    const type =
      body.type
        ?.trim()
        .toUpperCase();

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !claim ||
      !type
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId, type, and claim are required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (!TYPES.has(type)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Invalid fact type.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      body.confidence !== undefined &&
      (
        typeof body.confidence !== "number" ||
        body.confidence < 0 ||
        body.confidence > 1
      )
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "confidence must be between 0 and 1.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    let sourceUrl:
      string | null =
      null;

    if (
      body.sourceUrl?.trim()
    ) {
      try {
        const parsed =
          new URL(
            body.sourceUrl.trim()
          );

        if (
          parsed.protocol !== "https:" &&
          parsed.protocol !== "http:"
        ) {
          throw new Error(
            "bad protocol"
          );
        }

        sourceUrl =
          parsed.toString();
      } catch {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "sourceUrl must be a valid HTTP or HTTPS URL.",
          },
          {
            status: 400,
          }
        );
      }
    }

    const fact =
      await prisma.businessFact.create({
        data: {
          businessId:
            website.businessId,

          type:
            type as BusinessFactType,

          claim,

          status:
            "PROPOSED",

          sourceUrl,

          sourceLabel:
            body.sourceLabel
              ?.trim() ||
            null,

          evidence:
            body.evidence
              ?.trim() ||
            null,

          confidence:
            body.confidence ??
            null,

          createdByUserId:
            user.id,

          events: {
            create: {
              action:
                "CREATED",

              actorUserId:
                user.id,

              nextStatus:
                "PROPOSED",
            },
          },
        },

        include: {
          events:
            true,
        },
      });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        fact,
      },
      {
        status: 201,
      }
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(
      "content/facts POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } = authErrorResponse(err);

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/facts/[id]/reject/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";


export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params: Promise<{
      id: string;
    }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?: string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    const fact =
      await prisma.businessFact.findUnique({
        where: {
          id,
        },
      });

    if (
      !fact ||
      fact.businessId !==
        website.businessId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Business fact not found.",
        },
        {
          status: 404,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      fact.status !==
      "PROPOSED"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Only PROPOSED facts can be rejected. Current status: ${fact.status}.`,
        },
        {
          status: 409,
        }
      );
    }

    const [
      updated,
    ] =
      await prisma.$transaction([
        prisma.businessFact.update({
          where: {
            id,
          },

          data: {
            status:
              "REJECTED",
          },
        }),

        prisma.businessFactEvent.create({
          data: {
            businessFactId:
              id,

            action:
              "REJECTED",

            actorUserId:
              user.id,

            previousStatus:
              fact.status,

            nextStatus:
              "REJECTED",
          },
        }),
      ]);

    return NextResponse.json({
      fact:
        updated,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(
      "content/facts/[id]/reject POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/facts/[id]/verify/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  hashEvidenceText,
} from "@/lib/content/evidence-drift";



export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params: Promise<{
      id: string;
    }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?: string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim();

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    const fact =
      await prisma.businessFact.findUnique({
        where: {
          id,
        },
      });

    if (
      !fact ||
      fact.businessId !==
        website.businessId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Business fact not found.",
        },
        {
          status: 404,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      fact.status ===
      "VERIFIED"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        fact,
      });
    }

    if (
      fact.status !==
      "PROPOSED"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Only PROPOSED facts can be verified. Current status: ${fact.status}.`,
        },
        {
          status: 409,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !fact.sourceUrl ||
      !fact.evidence?.trim()
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Source URL and evidence are required before a business fact can be verified.",
        },
        {
          status: 409,
        }
      );
    }

    const [
      updated,
    ] =
      await prisma.$transaction([
        prisma.businessFact.update({
          where: {
            id,
          },

          data: {
            status:
              "VERIFIED",

            verifiedAt:
              new Date(),

            verifiedByUserId:
              user.id,

              evidenceStatus:
                "UNCHECKED",

              evidenceHash:
                hashEvidenceText(
                  fact.evidence ??
                    ""
                ),

              sourceContentHash:
                null,

              lastEvidenceCheckedAt:
                null,

              lastEvidenceSeenAt:
                null,

              evidenceCheckError:
                null,

          },
        }),

        prisma.businessFactEvent.create({
          data: {
            businessFactId:
              id,

            action:
              "VERIFIED",

            actorUserId:
              user.id,

            previousStatus:
              fact.status,

            nextStatus:
              "VERIFIED",
          },
        }),
      ]);

    return NextResponse.json({
      fact:
        updated,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(
      "content/facts/[id]/verify POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/briefs/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { createContentBrief } from "@/lib/content/service";
import { geminiGenerator } from "@/lib/content/gemini-generator";
import { getContentBriefs } from "@/lib/content/repository";
import {
  parseOptionalExplicitSearchMissionInput,
} from "@/lib/content/search-mission-ingress";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();

    const missionIngress =
      parseOptionalExplicitSearchMissionInput(
        (
          body as {
            searchMission?: unknown;
          }
        ).searchMission
      );

    if (
      !missionIngress.ok
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            missionIngress.error,

          code:
            missionIngress.code,
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    const searchMission =
      missionIngress.value;
    const { websiteId, opportunityId } = body as { websiteId?: string; opportunityId?: string };

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const website = await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    let topic: string;
    let reason: string;
    let recommendation: string;

    if (opportunityId) {
      const opportunity = await prisma.opportunity.findUnique({ where: { id: opportunityId } });
      if (!opportunity) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "Opportunity not found." }, { status: 404 });
      }
      topic = opportunity.title;
      reason = opportunity.reason;
      recommendation = opportunity.recommendation;
    } else {
      const { topic: bodyTopic, reason: bodyReason, recommendation: bodyRecommendation } = body as {
        topic?: string;
        reason?: string;
        recommendation?: string;
      };
      if (!bodyTopic || !bodyReason || !bodyRecommendation) {
        return NextResponse.json({ error: "topic, reason and recommendation are required when no opportunityId is given." }, { status: 400 });
      }
      topic = bodyTopic;
      reason = bodyReason;
      recommendation = bodyRecommendation;
    }

    const business = await prisma.business.findUnique({ where: { id: website.businessId } });
    if (!business) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Business not found for this website." }, { status: 500 });
    }

    const brief = await createContentBrief(geminiGenerator, {
      websiteId,
      opportunityId,
      topic,
      reason,
      recommendation,
      businessName: business.name,
      websiteUrl: website.url,

      searchMission,});

    return NextResponse.json({ brief }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (err) {
    const providerMessage =
      err instanceof Error
        ? err.message
        : "";

    const providerMatch =
      /(?:Gemini|Groq) API error (429|500|502|503|504)\b/.exec(
        providerMessage
      );

    if (providerMatch) {
      const providerStatus =
        Number(
          providerMatch[1]
        );

      console.warn(
        "content/briefs POST error provider failure",
        {
          status:
            providerStatus,
        }
      );

      if (
        providerStatus ===
        429
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "AI provider rate limit or quota was reached. Check provider quota/billing and try again.",

            code:
              "CONTENT_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMIT",

            retryable:
              true,
          },
          {
            status:
              429,

            headers: {
              "Retry-After":
                "30",
            },
          }
        );
      }

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "AI generation is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.",

          code:
            "CONTENT_PROVIDER_TEMPORARY",

          retryable:
            true,
        },
        {
          status:
            503,

          headers: {
            "Retry-After":
              "15",
          },
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      /AbortError|aborted|timeout/i.test(
        providerMessage
      )
    ) {
      console.warn(
        "content/briefs POST error provider timeout"
      );

      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "AI generation timed out. Please try again.",

          code:
            "CONTENT_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT",

          retryable:
            true,
        },
        {
          status:
            504,

          headers: {
            "Retry-After":
              "10",
          },
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "content/briefs POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body:
        errBody,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      errBody,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const websiteId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");
    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId query parameter is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const page = Number(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("page") ?? "1");
    const limit = Number(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("limit") ?? "25");

    const result = await getContentBriefs(websiteId, page, limit);
    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("content/briefs GET error", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

    );

      status,

      authErrorResponse(
        err

      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/content/strategy/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  buildContentStrategy,
} from "@/lib/content/seo-strategy";


export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim();

    const topic =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("topic")
        ?.trim();

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !topic
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId and topic are required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          business: {
            select: {
              name:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!website) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found.",
        },
        {
          status: 404,
        }
      );
    }

    const strategy =
      await buildContentStrategy({
        websiteId,
        topic,

        businessName:
          website.business
            .name,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      strategy,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[content strategy]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown strategy error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not build content strategy.",
      },
      {
        status: 500,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/workspaces/mine/route.ts ----------
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const membership = await prisma.workspaceMember.findFirst({
      where: { userId: user.id },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
    });
    if (!membership) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "No workspace found." }, { status: 404 });
    }
    return NextResponse.json({ workspaceId: membership.workspaceId });
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  verifyOAuthState,
  InvalidStateError,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  exchangeCodeForTokens,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  upsertGoogleAnalyticsConnection,
} from "@/lib/integrations/provider-connection-service";

function callbackUrl(
  req: NextRequest
): string {
  return (
    process.env
      .GOOGLE_GA4_REDIRECT_URI
      ?.trim() ||
    new URL(
      "/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback",
      req.url
    ).toString()
  );
}

function safeReturnTo(
  value:
    string | undefined
): string {
  if (
    value &&
    value.startsWith("/") &&
    !value.startsWith("//")
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return "/dashboard/audience?ga4=connected";
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const code =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("code");

    const state =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("state");

    const oauthError =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get("error");

    if (oauthError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Google OAuth error: ${oauthError}`,
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !code ||
      !state
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Missing code or state.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    let statePayload;

    try {
      statePayload =
        verifyOAuthState(
          state
        );
    } catch (error) {
      if (
        error instanceof
        InvalidStateError
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              error.message,
          },
          {
            status: 400,
          }
        );
      }

      throw error;
    }

    if (
      statePayload.userId !==
      user.id
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "State does not match authenticated user.",
        },
        {
          status: 403,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      statePayload.workspaceId
    );

    const tokens =
      await exchangeCodeForTokens(
        code,
        callbackUrl(req)
      );

    await upsertGoogleAnalyticsConnection(
      statePayload.workspaceId,
      tokens
    );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      new URL(
        safeReturnTo(
          statePayload.returnTo
        ),
        req.url
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "[GA4 callback error]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google-analytics/connect/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  createOAuthState,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  buildGoogleAuthUrl,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  GA4_OAUTH_SCOPES,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/constants";

function callbackUrl(
  req: NextRequest
): string {
  return (
    process.env
      .GOOGLE_GA4_REDIRECT_URI
      ?.trim() ||
    new URL(
      "/api/integrations/google-analytics/callback",
      req.url
    ).toString()
  );
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const workspaceId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "workspaceId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl
        .searchParams
        .get(
          "websiteId"
        )
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      workspaceId
    );

    if (websiteId) {
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );
    }

    const returnTo =
      websiteId
        ? `/dashboard/audience?websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}&ga4=connected`
        : "/dashboard/audience?ga4=connected";

    const state =
      createOAuthState(
        user.id,
        workspaceId,
        returnTo
      );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      buildGoogleAuthUrl(
        state,
        GA4_OAUTH_SCOPES,
        callbackUrl(req)
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

  value:
    string | undefined
): string {

    value &&
    value.startsWith("/") &&
    !value.startsWith("//")
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return "/dashboard/audience?gsc=connected";
}


export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const code =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("code");

    const state =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("state");

    const oauthError =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("error");

    if (oauthError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            `Google OAuth error: ${oauthError}`,
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !code ||
      !state
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Missing code or state.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    try {
      statePayload =
        verifyOAuthState(
          state
        );

      user.id,

    );

      await exchangeCodeForTokens(
        code
      );

      statePayload.workspaceId,

    );

      new URL(
        safeReturnTo(
          statePayload.returnTo
        ),

      )

        : "Unknown Google OAuth error"
    );

      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/google/connect/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  createOAuthState,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/state";

import {
  buildGoogleAuthUrl,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/oauth-client";

import {
  GSC_OAUTH_SCOPES,
} from "@/lib/integrations/google/constants";


export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    const requestedWorkspaceId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("workspaceId")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    let workspaceId:
      string | null =
        requestedWorkspaceId;

    if (websiteId) {
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

      const website =
        await prisma.website.findUnique({
          where: {
            id:
              websiteId,
          },

          select: {
            business: {
              select: {
                workspaceId:
                  true,
              },
            },
          },
        });

      if (!website) {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "Website not found.",
          },
          {
            status: 404,
          }
        );
      }

      workspaceId =
        website.business
          .workspaceId;
    }

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId or workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (!websiteId) {
      await requireWorkspaceAccess(
        user.id,
        workspaceId
      );
    }

    const returnTo =
      websiteId
        ? `/dashboard/audience?websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}&gsc=connected`
        : "/dashboard/audience?gsc=connected";

    const state =
      createOAuthState(
        user.id,
        workspaceId,
        returnTo
      );

    return NextResponse.redirect(
      buildGoogleAuthUrl(
        state,
        GSC_OAUTH_SCOPES
      )
    );
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/cloudflare/sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { syncCloudflareTraffic } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/sync";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, days = 7 } = body;

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId is required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (
      typeof days !== "number" ||
      !Number.isInteger(days) ||
      days < 1 ||
      days > 90
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "days must be an integer between 1 and 90" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const until = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const sinceDate = new Date();
    sinceDate.setDate(sinceDate.getDate() - (days - 1));
    const since = sinceDate.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    const result = await syncCloudflareTraffic({ websiteId, since, until });

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      connectionId: result.connectionId,
      requestedDays: result.requestedDays,
      syncedDays: result.syncedDays,
      skippedDays: result.skippedDays,
      availableFrom: result.availableFrom,
      availableTo: result.availableTo,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Cloudflare sync error:", error);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(error);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/integrations/cloudflare/connect/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";

  requireUser,

} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { upsertCloudflareConnection } from "@/lib/integrations/cloudflare/connection-service";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, zoneId, apiToken } = body;

    if (!websiteId || !zoneId || !apiToken) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "websiteId, zoneId, and apiToken are required" },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const connection = await upsertCloudflareConnection(websiteId, zoneId, apiToken);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      connectionId: connection.id,
      zoneId: connection.zoneId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Cloudflare connect error:", error);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(error);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/cms/fixes/route.ts ----------

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {

    if (!workspaceId) {

    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }

---------- src/app/api/cms/apply-fix/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  applyFix,
} from "@/lib/cms/service";

import {
  getProviderForType,
} from "@/lib/cms/registry";

import {
  getCMSConnection,
} from "@/lib/cms/repository";

import {
  UnsupportedFixTypeError,
  CMSNotConnectedError,
} from "@/lib/cms/provider";

import type {
  FixType,
} from "@/lib/cms/types";

import {
  assertOutcomeActionExecutable,
  markOutcomeActionExecuted,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-service";

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req.json();

    const {
      websiteId,
      opportunityId,
      issueId,
      pageUrl,
      fixType,
      newValue,
      outcomeActionId:
        rawOutcomeActionId,
    } = body as {
      websiteId?: string;
      opportunityId?: string;
      issueId?: string;
      pageUrl?: string;
      fixType?: FixType;
      newValue?: string;
      outcomeActionId?: string;
    };

    const outcomeActionId =
      typeof rawOutcomeActionId ===
        "string"
        ? rawOutcomeActionId.trim()
        : "";

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !pageUrl ||
      !fixType ||
      !newValue
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId, pageUrl, fixType and newValue are required.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    if (
      outcomeActionId
    ) {
      try {
        await assertOutcomeActionExecutable({
          workspaceId:
            website.business.workspaceId,

          websiteId,

          actionId:
            outcomeActionId,

          executionKind:
            "CMS_FIX",

          requestedPageUrl:
            pageUrl,
        });
      } catch {
        return NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "This Growth OS action cannot be linked to this website execution.",
          },
          {
            status:
              409,
          }
        );
      }
    }

    const connection =
      await getCMSConnection(
        websiteId
      );

    if (
      !connection
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "CMS is not connected for this website",
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    const provider =
      getProviderForType(
        connection.cmsType
      );

    const result =
      await applyFix(
        provider,
        {
          websiteId,
          opportunityId,
          issueId,
          pageUrl,
          fixType,
          newValue,
        }
      );

    let outcomeLink:
      | {
          status:
            "EXECUTED";
          actionId:
            string;
        }
      | {
          status:
            "NOT_EXECUTED";
          actionId:
            string;
        }
      | {
          status:
            "FAILED_TO_RECORD";
          actionId:
            string;
        }
      | null =
      null;

    if (
      outcomeActionId
    ) {
      if (
        result.status ===
        "SUCCESS"
      ) {
        try {
          await markOutcomeActionExecuted({
            workspaceId:
              website.business.workspaceId,

            websiteId,

            actionId:
              outcomeActionId,

            executionKind:
              "CMS_FIX",

            requestedPageUrl:
              pageUrl,

            appliedFixId:
              result.id,
          });

          outcomeLink = {
            status:
              "EXECUTED",

            actionId:
              outcomeActionId,
          };
        } catch (
          linkError
        ) {
          console.error(
            "cms/apply-fix outcome link error",
            linkError
          );

          outcomeLink = {
            status:
              "FAILED_TO_RECORD",

            actionId:
              outcomeActionId,
          };
        }
      } else {
        outcomeLink = {
          status:
            "NOT_EXECUTED",

          actionId:
            outcomeActionId,
        };
      }
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      outcomeLink
        ? {
            fix:
              result,

            outcomeLink,
          }
        : {
            fix:
              result,
          },
      {
        status:
          201,
      }
    );
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    if (
      err instanceof
      UnsupportedFixTypeError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            err.message,
        },
        {
          status:
            422,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      err instanceof
      CMSNotConnectedError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            err.message,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "cms/apply-fix POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body:
        errBody,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      errBody,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/cms/connect/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { upsertCMSConnection, getCMSConnection, markCMSConnectionStatus } from "@/lib/cms/repository";
import { getProviderForType } from "@/lib/cms/registry";
import type { CMSType } from "@/lib/cms/types";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, cmsType, siteUrl, username, password } = body as {
      websiteId?: string;
      cmsType?: CMSType;
      siteUrl?: string;
      username?: string;
      password?: string;
    };

    if (!websiteId || !cmsType || !siteUrl || !password) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId, cmsType, siteUrl and password (or API key) are required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    if (cmsType === "WORDPRESS" && !username) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "username is required for WordPress connections." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    await upsertCMSConnection({ websiteId, cmsType, siteUrl, username, password });

    const provider = getProviderForType(cmsType);
    const verification = await provider.verifyCredentials(siteUrl, username ?? "", password);

    const connection = verification.valid
      ? await markCMSConnectionStatus(websiteId, "CONNECTED")
      : await markCMSConnectionStatus(websiteId, "ERROR", verification.error ?? undefined);

    return NextResponse.json({ connection, verified: verification.valid }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("cms/connect POST error", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const websiteId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");
    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId query parameter is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const connection = await getCMSConnection(websiteId);
    return NextResponse.json({ connection });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("cms/connect GET error", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/analyses/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { crawlWebsite } from "@/lib/crawler/crawler";
import { runSEORules } from "@/lib/seo-engine/rules";
import { calculateScore, getSeverityCounts } from "@/lib/seo-engine/scoring";
import { generateOpportunities } from "@/lib/opportunities";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId } = body as { websiteId?: string };

    if (!websiteId || typeof websiteId !== "string") {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const website = await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const analysis = await prisma.analysis.create({
      data: { websiteId, status: "RUNNING", startedAt: new Date() },
    });

    console.log(`[analysis] Started: ${analysis.id} for ${website.url}`);

    runAnalysis(analysis.id, website.url).catch(console.error);

    return NextResponse.json({ analysisId: analysis.id }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[analysis] POST error:", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

async function runAnalysis(analysisId: string, url: string) {
  try {
    console.log(`[analysis] Crawling: ${url}`);
    const crawledPages = await crawlWebsite(url, { maxPages: 25, concurrency: 3 });
    console.log(`[analysis] Crawled ${crawledPages.length} pages`);

    for (const page of crawledPages) {
      try {
        await prisma.page.create({
          data: {
            url: page.url,
            statusCode: page.statusCode,
            title: page.parsed?.title ?? null,
            metaDescription: page.parsed?.metaDescription ?? null,
            h1: page.parsed?.h1 ?? null,
            h2s: page.parsed?.h2s ?? [],
            canonical: page.parsed?.canonical ?? null,
            robots: page.parsed?.robots ?? null,
            wordCount: page.parsed?.wordCount ?? null,
            internalLinks: page.parsed?.internalLinks?.length ?? null,
            externalLinks: page.parsed?.externalLinks?.length ?? null,
            imagesCount: page.parsed?.imagesCount ?? null,
            imagesWithoutAlt: page.parsed?.imagesWithoutAlt ?? null,
            hasJsonLd: page.parsed?.hasJsonLd ?? false,
            responseTime: page.responseTime,
            depth: page.depth,
            analysisId,
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[analysis] Failed to store page ${page.url}:`, err);
      }
    }

    const issues = runSEORules(crawledPages);
    const score = calculateScore(issues);
    const counts = getSeverityCounts(issues);
    const opportunities = generateOpportunities(issues);

    console.log(`[analysis] Issues: ${issues.length} (CRITICAL:${counts.CRITICAL} HIGH:${counts.HIGH} MEDIUM:${counts.MEDIUM} LOW:${counts.LOW})`);
    console.log(`[analysis] Score: ${score.overallScore}`);
    console.log(`[analysis] Opportunities: ${opportunities.length}`);

    for (const issue of issues) {
      try {
        await prisma.sEOIssue.create({
          data: {
            type: issue.type,
            severity: issue.severity,
            pageUrl: issue.pageUrl,
            description: issue.description,
            evidence: issue.evidence,
            recommendation: issue.recommendation,
            analysisId,
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[analysis] Failed to store issue:`, err);
      }
    }

    for (const opp of opportunities) {
      try {
        await prisma.opportunity.create({
          data: {
            title: opp.title,
            type: opp.type,
            priority: opp.priority,
            score: opp.score,
            reason: opp.reason,
            recommendation: opp.recommendation,
            affectedPages: opp.affectedPages,
            analysisId,
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`[analysis] Failed to store opportunity:`, err);
      }
    }

    await prisma.analysis.update({
      where: { id: analysisId },
      data: {
        status: "COMPLETED",
        completedAt: new Date(),
        overallScore: score.overallScore,
        technicalScore: score.technicalScore,
        contentScore: score.contentScore,
        structureScore: score.structureScore,
        indexabilityScore: score.indexabilityScore,
        pagesCount: crawledPages.length,
      },
    });

    console.log(`[analysis] Completed: ${analysisId}`);
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
    console.error(`[analysis] Failed: ${analysisId}`, err);
    await prisma.analysis.update({
      where: { id: analysisId },
      data: { status: "FAILED", errorMessage: message },
    });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/analyses/[id]/opportunities/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
