---------- src/app/api/analyses/[id]/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireAnalysisAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function GET(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const user = await requireUser();

    await requireAnalysisAccess(user.id, id);

    const analysis = await prisma.analysis.findUnique({
      where: { id },
      include: {
        website: { include: { business: true } },
        _count: {
          select: { pages: true, issues: true, opportunities: true },
        },
      },
    });

    if (!analysis) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Analysis not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ analysis });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/analyses/[id]/issues/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requireUser, requireAnalysisAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

const SEVERITY_ORDER: Record<string, number> = {
  CRITICAL: 0,
  HIGH: 1,
  MEDIUM: 2,
  LOW: 3,
};

export async function GET(
  _req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const user = await requireUser();

    await requireAnalysisAccess(user.id, id);

    const issues = await prisma.sEOIssue.findMany({
      where: { analysisId: id },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "asc" },
    });

    const sorted = issues.sort(
      (a, b) =>
        (SEVERITY_ORDER[a.severity] ?? 9) - (SEVERITY_ORDER[b.severity] ?? 9)
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ issues: sorted });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error(err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/audience/ga4/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getGa4Context,
  getGa4AccessToken,
  listAccessibleGa4Properties,
  verifyGa4PropertyAccess,
  saveGa4Property,
  getGa4Intelligence,
} from "@/lib/audience/ga4-intelligence";

const ALLOWED_DAYS =
  new Set([
    1,
    7,
    15,
    30,
  ]);

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("websiteId")
        ?.trim();

    const days =
      Number(
        req.nextUrl.searchParams
          .get("days") ??
        "30"
      );

    const mode =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("mode") ??
      "history";

    const country =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get("country")
        ?.trim() ||
      null;

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    if (
      !ALLOWED_DAYS.has(
        days
      )
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "days must be 1, 7, 15, or 30.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const context =
      await getGa4Context(
        websiteId
      );

    const token =
      await getGa4AccessToken(
        context.workspaceId
      );

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        connected:
          false,

        source:
          "GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_4",

        connectUrl:
          `/api/integrations/google-analytics/connect?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(
            context.workspaceId
          )}&websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}`,
      });
    }

    if (
      !context.ga4PropertyId
    ) {
      const properties =
        await listAccessibleGa4Properties(
          token
        );

      return NextResponse.json({
        connected:
          true,

        source:
          "GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_4",

        needsProperty:
          true,

        properties,
      });
    }

    const report =
      await getGa4Intelligence({
        propertyId:
          context.ga4PropertyId,

        token,

        days,

        mode,

        country,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      connected:
        true,

      source:
        "GOOGLE_ANALYTICS_4",

      needsProperty:
        false,

      ...report,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    const message =
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error";

    console.error(
      "[GA4 intelligence]",
      message
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          `Google Analytics request failed: ${message}`,
      },
      {
        status: 502,
      }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?:
          string;

        propertyId?:
          string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId
        ?.trim();

    const propertyId =
      body.propertyId
        ?.trim()
        .replace(
          /^properties\//,
          ""
        );

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !propertyId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId and propertyId are required.",
        },
        {
          status: 400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const context =
      await getGa4Context(
        websiteId
      );

    const token =
      await getGa4AccessToken(
        context.workspaceId
      );

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Google Analytics is not connected.",
        },
        {
          status: 409,
        }
      );
    }

    await verifyGa4PropertyAccess(
      propertyId,
      token
    );

    await saveGa4Property(
      websiteId,
      propertyId
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      success:
        true,

      propertyId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        error
      );

    if (
      status !== 500
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        {
          status,
        }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "Could not save GA4 property.",
      },
      {
        status: 502,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/audience/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { getAudienceSegments } from "@/lib/audience/repository";

function parsePositiveInt(value: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const n = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : undefined;
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const websiteId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");
    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const page = parsePositiveInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("page")) ?? 1;
    const limit = parsePositiveInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("limit")) ?? 25;

    const result = await getAudienceSegments(websiteId, { page, limit });
    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/audience/search-queries/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import {
  gscProvider,
  ProviderNotConnectedError,
  SitePermissionError,
} from "@/lib/keywords/gsc-provider";
import type { KeywordProvenance } from "@/lib/keywords/types";

const ALLOWED_DAYS =
  new Set([7, 15, 30]);

const MAX_RESULTS = 50;

interface Bucket {
  query: string;
  clicks: number;
  impressions: number;
  weightedPosition: number;
  positionWeight: number;
  pages: Map<
    string,
    {
      page: string;
      clicks: number;
      impressions: number;
    }
  >;
}

function round(
  value: number,
  digits = 1
) {
  const factor = 10 ** digits;

  return (
    Math.round(value * factor) /
    factor
  );
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get(
        "websiteId"
      );

    const days = Number(
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get(
        "days"
      ) ?? "7"
    );

    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    if (!ALLOWED_DAYS.has(days)) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "days must be 7, 15, or 30",
        },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id: websiteId,
        },
        select: {
          url: true,
          business: {
            select: {
              workspaceId: true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    if (!website) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found",
        },
        { status: 404 }
      );
    }

    const workspaceId =
      website.business.workspaceId;

    const connection =
      await gscProvider.getConnectionStatus(
        workspaceId
      );

    if (
      connection.status !==
      "CONNECTED"
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        connected: false,
        source:
          "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",
        days,
        range: null,
        summary: null,
        queries: [],

        state:
          "DISCONNECTED",

        connectUrl:
          `/api/integrations/google/connect?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(
            workspaceId
          )}&websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
            websiteId
          )}`,
      });
    }

    const now = new Date();

    const endDate = new Date(
      Date.UTC(
        now.getUTCFullYear(),
        now.getUTCMonth(),
        now.getUTCDate()
      )
    );

    endDate.setUTCDate(
      endDate.getUTCDate() - 1
    );

    const startDate =
      new Date(endDate);

    startDate.setUTCDate(
      startDate.getUTCDate() -
        (days - 1)
    );

    const performanceResult =
      await (async () => {
        try {
          const rows =
            await gscProvider.fetchSearchPerformance({
              workspaceId,
              siteUrl:
                website.url,
              startDate,
              endDate,
            });

          return {
            ok:
              true as const,
            rows,
          };
        } catch (error) {
          return {
            ok:
              false as const,
            error,
          };
        }
      })();

    if (
      !performanceResult.ok
    ) {
      const providerError =
        performanceResult.error;

      const connectUrl =
        `/api/integrations/google/connect?workspaceId=${encodeURIComponent(
          workspaceId
        )}&websiteId=${encodeURIComponent(
          websiteId
        )}`;

      if (
        providerError instanceof
        SitePermissionError
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          connected:
            true,

          source:
            "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

          days,

          range: {
            start:
              startDate
                .toISOString()
                .slice(0, 10),

            end:
              endDate
                .toISOString()
                .slice(0, 10),
          },

          summary:
            null,

          queries:
            [],

          state:
            "PROPERTY_UNAVAILABLE",

          message:
            "The connected Google account does not have Search Console access to this website.",

          connectUrl,
        });
      }

      const providerMessage =
        providerError instanceof Error
          ? providerError.message
          : "";

      const reconnectRequired =
        providerError instanceof
          ProviderNotConnectedError ||
        /invalid_grant|invalid credentials|unauthorized|authentication|refresh token|token has been expired|token has been revoked/i.test(
          providerMessage
        );

      if (
        reconnectRequired
      ) {
        return NextResponse.json({
          connected:
            false,

          source:
            "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

          days,

          range:
            null,

          summary:
            null,

          queries:
            [],

          state:
            "RECONNECT_REQUIRED",

          message:
            "Google Search Console authorization needs to be refreshed.",

          connectUrl,
        });
      }

      console.error(
        "[audience search queries provider]",
        providerMessage ||
          "Unknown Search Console provider error"
      );

      return NextResponse.json({
        connected:
          true,

        source:
          "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

        days,

        range: {
          start:
            startDate
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 10),

          end:
            endDate
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 10),
        },

        summary:
          null,

        queries:
          [],

        state:
          "TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE",

        message:
          "Search Console is temporarily unavailable.",

        connectUrl,
      });
    }

    const rawRows =
      performanceResult.rows;

    const provenance:
      KeywordProvenance = {
        provider:
          "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",
        retrievedAt: new Date(),
        dateRangeStart:
          startDate,
        dateRangeEnd:
          endDate,
        country: "us",
        device: "DESKTOP",
      };

    const normalized =
      gscProvider.normalizeRows(
        rawRows,
        provenance
      );

    const buckets =
      new Map<string, Bucket>();

    for (
      const {
        keyword,
        snapshot,
      } of normalized
    ) {
      const query =
        keyword.text.trim();

      if (!query) continue;

      const clicks =
        Math.max(
          0,
          Number(
            snapshot.clicks ?? 0
          )
        );

      const impressions =
        Math.max(
          0,
          Number(
            snapshot.impressions ?? 0
          )
        );

      const position =
        snapshot.position == null
          ? null
          : Number(
              snapshot.position
            );

      const page =
        snapshot.pageUrl?.trim() ??
        "";

      let bucket =
        buckets.get(query);

      if (!bucket) {
        bucket = {
          query,
          clicks: 0,
          impressions: 0,
          weightedPosition: 0,
          positionWeight: 0,
          pages: new Map(),
        };

        buckets.set(
          query,
          bucket
        );
      }

      bucket.clicks += clicks;
      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      if (
        position !== null &&
        Number.isFinite(position)
      ) {
        const weight =
          Math.max(
            1,
            impressions
          );

        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          position * weight;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          weight;
      }

      if (page) {
        const existing =
          bucket.pages.get(page) ?? {
            page,
            clicks: 0,
            impressions: 0,
          };

        existing.clicks +=
          clicks;

        existing.impressions +=
          impressions;

        bucket.pages.set(
          page,
          existing
        );
      }
    }

    const queries =
      [...buckets.values()]
        .map((bucket) => {
          const topPage =
            [...bucket.pages.values()]
              .sort(
                (a, b) =>
                  b.clicks -
                    a.clicks ||
                  b.impressions -
                    a.impressions
              )[0]?.page ?? null;

          const ctr =
            bucket.impressions > 0
              ? (
                  bucket.clicks /
                  bucket.impressions
                ) * 100
              : 0;

          const avgPosition =
            bucket.positionWeight > 0
              ? (
                  bucket.weightedPosition /
                  bucket.positionWeight
                )
              : null;

          return {
            query:
              bucket.query,

            clicks:
              bucket.clicks,

            impressions:
              bucket.impressions,

            ctr:
              round(ctr, 1),

            avgPosition:
              avgPosition === null
                ? null
                : round(
                    avgPosition,
                    1
                  ),

            topPage,
          };
        })
        .sort(
          (a, b) =>
            b.clicks -
              a.clicks ||
            b.impressions -
              a.impressions ||
            (a.avgPosition ??
              999) -
              (b.avgPosition ??
                999)
        );

    const clicks =
      queries.reduce(
        (sum, row) =>
          sum + row.clicks,
        0
      );

    const impressions =
      queries.reduce(
        (sum, row) =>
          sum +
          row.impressions,
        0
      );

    const weighted =
      [...buckets.values()]
        .reduce(
          (acc, bucket) => {
            acc.total +=
              bucket.weightedPosition;

            acc.weight +=
              bucket.positionWeight;

            return acc;
          },
          {
            total: 0,
            weight: 0,
          }
        );

    return NextResponse.json({
      connected: true,

      source:
        "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

      days,

      range: {
        start:
          startDate
            .toISOString()
            .slice(0, 10),

        end:
          endDate
            .toISOString()
            .slice(0, 10),
      },

      summary: {
        clicks,

        impressions,

        ctr:
          impressions > 0
            ? round(
                (
                  clicks /
                  impressions
                ) * 100,
                1
              )
            : 0,

        avgPosition:
          weighted.weight > 0
            ? round(
                weighted.total /
                  weighted.weight,
                1
              )
            : null,

        uniqueQueries:
          queries.length,
      },

      queries:
        queries.slice(
          0,
          MAX_RESULTS
        ),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(error);

    if (status !== 500) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        body,
        { status }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[audience/search-queries]",
      error instanceof Error
        ? error.message
        : "Unknown error"
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Could not load Google Search Console data.",
      },
      { status: 502 }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/audience/segments/[id]/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { getSegmentById, getAudienceSnapshots } from "@/lib/audience/repository";

function parsePositiveInt(value: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const n = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : undefined;
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { id } = await params;
    const user = await requireUser();

    const segment = await getSegmentById(id);
    if (!segment) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Segment not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, segment.websiteId);

    const page = parsePositiveInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("page")) ?? 1;
    const limit = parsePositiveInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("limit")) ?? 25;

    const result = await getAudienceSnapshots(id, { page, limit });
    return NextResponse.json({ segment, ...result });
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/seo/suggestions/[issueId]/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { generateSuggestion } from "@/lib/seo/suggestions";

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ issueId: string }> }
) {
  try {
    const { issueId } = await params;
    const user = await requireUser();

    const issue = await prisma.sEOIssue.findUnique({
      where: { id: issueId },
      include: { analysis: { include: { website: true } } },
    });

    if (!issue) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Issue not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, issue.analysis.websiteId);

    const suggestion = generateSuggestion(issue);
    if (!suggestion) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "No automated suggestion available for this issue type." },
        { status: 422 }
      );
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ issueId, suggestion });
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/live-rank/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  authErrorResponse,
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  deriveSearchSeed,
} from "@/lib/keywords/seed-utils";

import {
  getCachedLiveRankReport,
  LiveRankRateLimitError,
  refreshLiveRankReport,
} from "@/lib/keywords/live-rank-service";

async function loadContext(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  const user =
    await requireUser();

  const workspaceId =
    req.nextUrl.searchParams
      .get(
        "workspaceId"
      )
      ?.trim();

  const websiteId =
    req.nextUrl.searchParams
      .get(
        "websiteId"
      )
      ?.trim();

  const market =
    (
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get(
        "market"
      ) ??
      "US"
    ).toUpperCase();

  if (
    !workspaceId ||
    !websiteId
  ) {
    return {
      error:
        NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "workspaceId and websiteId are required.",
          },
          {
            status:
              400,
          }
        ),
    };
  }

  await requireWorkspaceAccess(
    user.id,
    workspaceId
  );

  const website =
    await prisma.website.findFirst({
      where: {
        id:
          websiteId,

        business: {
          workspaceId,
        },
      },

      include: {
        business:
          true,
      },
    });

  if (!website) {
    return {
      error:
        NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "Website not found.",
          },
          {
            status:
              404,
          }
        ),
    };
  }

  const industry =
    website.business.industry?.trim() ??
    "";

  const seed =
    industry &&
    !industry.startsWith(
      "http"
    )
      ? deriveSearchSeed(
          industry
        )
      : "";

  if (!seed) {
    return {
      error:
        NextResponse.json(
          {
            error:
              "Add your business industry before checking search positions.",
          },
          {
            status:
              400,
          }
        ),
    };
  }

  return {
    workspaceId,
    website,
    seed,
    market,
  };
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const context =
      await loadContext(
        req
      );

    if (
      "error" in
      context
    ) {
      return context.error;
    }

    const report =
      await getCachedLiveRankReport({
        siteUrl:
          context.website.url,
        seed:
          context.seed,
        market:
          context.market,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      report,
      cached:
        Boolean(
          report
        ),
    });
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const context =
      await loadContext(
        req
      );

    if (
      "error" in
      context
    ) {
      return context.error;
    }

    const report =
      await refreshLiveRankReport({
        workspaceId:
          context.workspaceId,
        siteUrl:
          context.website.url,
        seed:
          context.seed,
        market:
          context.market,
      });

    return NextResponse.json({
      report,
      cached:
        false,
    });
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    if (
      err instanceof
      LiveRankRateLimitError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "You have already refreshed live positions 3 times today. Try again tomorrow.",
        },
        {
          status:
            429,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[keywords/live-rank]",
      err instanceof Error
        ? err.message
        : err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/market-breakdown/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import {
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import {
  getMarketBreakdown,
} from "@/lib/keywords/market-breakdown";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const workspaceId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");

    const keyword =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("keyword");

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "workspaceId is required." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    if (!keyword?.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "keyword is required." },
        { status: 400 },
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const markets = await getMarketBreakdown(keyword.trim());

    return NextResponse.json({
      keyword: keyword.trim(),
      markets,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: err.message },
        { status: 502 },
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[keywords/market-breakdown error]",
      err,
    );

    const { status, body } =
      authErrorResponse(err);

    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/volume-sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { updateKeywordVolumes } from "@/lib/keywords/volume-service";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { workspaceId, forceRefresh, market, language } = body as {
      workspaceId?: string;
      forceRefresh?: boolean;
      market?: string;
      language?: string;
    };

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const result = await updateKeywordVolumes(
      workspaceId,
      forceRefresh ?? false,
      market ?? "US",
      language ?? "English",
    );
    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("[keywords/volume-sync error]", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/[keywordId]/snapshots/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { getKeywordSnapshots } from "@/lib/keywords/repository";

function parsePositiveInt(value: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const n = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : undefined;
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ keywordId: string }> }) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const { keywordId } = await params;
    const workspaceId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");
    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }
    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    // Ownership check -- never trust a keywordId from the browser alone.
    const keyword = await prisma.keyword.findUnique({ where: { id: keywordId } });
    if (!keyword || keyword.workspaceId !== workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Keyword not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    const days = parsePositiveInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("days")) ?? 90;
    const snapshots = await getKeywordSnapshots(keywordId, days);

    return NextResponse.json({
      keyword: keyword.text,
      snapshots: snapshots.map((s) => ({
        date: s.date.toISOString().slice(0, 10),
        position: s.position !== null ? Number(s.position) : null,
        clicks: s.clicks,
        impressions: s.impressions,
        ctr: s.ctr !== null ? Number(s.ctr) : null,
      })),
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[keywords/snapshots error]", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/signals/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { detectSignals } from "@/lib/keywords/signals";
import type { ProviderType } from "@/lib/keywords/types";

const SIGNAL_LOOKBACK_DAYS = 60; // two DECLINING_DAYS windows plus buffer
const SIGNALS_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE = 200;
const SIGNALS_MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 500;

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const workspaceId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");
    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }
    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const page = Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("page") ?? "1", 10) || 1);
    const limit = Math.min(
      SIGNALS_MAX_PAGE_SIZE,
      Math.max(
        1,
        Number.parseInt(req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("limit") ?? String(SIGNALS_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE), 10) ||
          SIGNALS_DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE
      )
    );

    const since = new Date();
    since.setDate(since.getDate() - SIGNAL_LOOKBACK_DAYS);

    const keywords = await prisma.keyword.findMany({
      where: { workspaceId },
      orderBy: { text: "asc" },
      skip: (page - 1) * limit,
      take: limit,
      include: {
        snapshots: {
          where: { date: { gte: since } },
          orderBy: { date: "asc" },
        },
      },
    });

    const signals = detectSignals(
      keywords.map((k) => ({
        id: k.id,
        workspaceId: k.workspaceId,
        text: k.text,
        normalized: k.normalized,
        snapshots: k.snapshots.map((s) => ({
          date: s.date,
          position: s.position !== null ? Number(s.position) : undefined,
          clicks: s.clicks ?? undefined,
          impressions: s.impressions ?? undefined,
          ctr: s.ctr !== null ? Number(s.ctr) : undefined,
          device: s.device,
          country: s.country,
          source: s.source as ProviderType,
          pageUrl: s.pageUrl ?? undefined,
          retrievedAt: s.retrievedAt,
        })),
      }))
    );

    return NextResponse.json({ signals, page, limit, keywordsScanned: keywords.length });
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/outcome-actions/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  getOpportunityDiagnosis,
} from "@/lib/keywords/opportunity-diagnosis";

import {
  buildOpportunityActionPlan,
} from "@/lib/keywords/opportunity-action-plan";

import {
  recordOutcomeAction,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-service";

import {
  findLatestOutcomeAction,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-repository";

import {
  normalizeOutcomeKeyword,
  shouldReuseRecentOutcomeAction,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-start";

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?: string;
        keyword?: string;
        market?: string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim() ??
      "";

    const keyword =
      body.keyword?.trim() ??
      "";

    const market =
      (
        body.market?.trim() ??
        ""
      ).toUpperCase();

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !keyword ||
      !market
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId, keyword and market are required.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    const normalizedKeyword =
      normalizeOutcomeKeyword(
        keyword
      );

    const latest =
      await findLatestOutcomeAction({
        workspaceId:
          website.business.workspaceId,

        websiteId,

        normalizedKeyword,

        market,
      });

    if (
      shouldReuseRecentOutcomeAction(
        latest
      )
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        action:
          latest,

        reused:
          true,
      });
    }

    const diagnosisResult =
      await getOpportunityDiagnosis({
        siteUrl:
          website.url,

        keyword,

        market,
      });

    const actionPlan =
      buildOpportunityActionPlan(
        diagnosisResult.diagnosis
      );

    const action =
      await recordOutcomeAction({
        workspaceId:
          website.business.workspaceId,

        websiteId,

        diagnosis:
          diagnosisResult.diagnosis,

        actionPlan,

        source:
          "GROWTH_OS_ACTION_PLAN_V1",
      });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        action,

        reused:
          false,
      },
      {
        status:
          201,
      }
    );
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    console.error(
      "keywords/outcome-actions POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/outcome-actions/[id]/create-brief/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
  authErrorResponse,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  createContentBrief,
} from "@/lib/content/service";

import {
  geminiGenerator,
} from "@/lib/content/gemini-generator";

import {
  getOutcomeActionById,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-repository";

import {
  evaluateOutcomeCreateBriefPermission,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-create-brief";

import {
  evaluateBusinessCompatibilityForWebsite,
} from "@/lib/keywords/business-compatibility";

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id:
          string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const {
      id,
    } =
      await params;

    const body =
      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?:
          string;
      };

    const websiteId =
      body.websiteId?.trim() ??
      "";

    if (
      !websiteId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId is required.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    const website =
      await requireWebsiteAccess(
        user.id,
        websiteId
      );

    const action =
      await getOutcomeActionById(
        website.business.workspaceId,
        id
      );

    if (
      !action ||
      action.websiteId !==
        websiteId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Growth OS action not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    const existing =
      await prisma.contentBrief.findUnique({
        where: {
          outcomeActionId:
            action.id,
        },
      });

    if (
      existing
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json({
        brief:
          existing,

        reused:
          true,
      });
    }

    const permission =
      evaluateOutcomeCreateBriefPermission({
        decision:
          action.decision,

        status:
          action.status,

        compatibilityStatus:
          action.compatibilityStatus,
      });

    if (
      !permission.allowed
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            permission.reason,

          compatibilityStatus:
            action.compatibilityStatus,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    const currentCompatibility =
      await evaluateBusinessCompatibilityForWebsite({
        websiteId,

        keyword:
          action.keywordText,
      });

    if (
      currentCompatibility.status !==
      action.compatibilityStatus
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Данные бизнеса изменились после создания плана. Обновите анализ перед выполнением.",

          storedCompatibilityStatus:
            action.compatibilityStatus,

          currentCompatibilityStatus:
            currentCompatibility.status,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    const educationalOnly =
      action.compatibilityStatus ===
      "EDUCATIONAL_ONLY";

    const recommendation =
      educationalOnly
        ? [
            "Create an educational page only.",
            "Do not imply that the business provides this product, service, or capability.",
            "Use only claims supported by verified Business Facts and the immutable evidence snapshot.",
          ].join(
            " "
          )
        : [
            "Create the dedicated page described by the Growth OS Outcome Action.",
            "Keep the primary keyword aligned exactly with the Outcome Action.",
            "Use only verified business claims and evidence-backed content.",
          ].join(
            " "
          );

    const brief =
      await createContentBrief(
        geminiGenerator,
        {
          websiteId,

          outcomeActionId:
            action.id,

          requiredPrimaryKeyword:
            action.keywordText,

          topic:
            action.keywordText,

          reason:
            action.rationale,

          recommendation,

          businessName:
            website.business.name,

          websiteUrl:
            website.url,
        }
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        brief,

        reused:
          false,

        compatibilityStatus:
          action.compatibilityStatus,
      },
      {
        status:
          201,
      }
    );
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    if (
      err instanceof Error &&
      err.message.startsWith(
        "Content plan primary keyword"
      )
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            err.message,
        },
        {
          status:
            409,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "outcome-actions/[id]/create-brief POST error",
      err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/outcome-actions/[id]/refresh-compatibility/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,

} from "next/server";

import {
  requireUser,

  refreshOutcomeActionCompatibility,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-revalidation";

export async function POST(
  req: NextRequest,
  {
    params,
  }: {
    params:
      Promise<{
        id:
          string;
      }>;
  }
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

      id,
    } =
      await params;

      await req.json() as {
        websiteId?:
          string;
      };

      body.websiteId?.trim() ??
      "";

    if (
      !websiteId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(

    err
  ) {

      status,
