---------- src/app/api/keywords/explore/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import { getCachedRelatedKeywords, RateLimitError } from "@/lib/keywords/suggestion-cache";
import { fetchTrendingQueries } from "@/lib/keywords/trends-provider";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const workspaceId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");
    const seed = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("seed");
    const limitParam = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("limit");
    const limit = limitParam ? Math.min(50, Math.max(1, Number.parseInt(limitParam, 10) || 50)) : 50;

    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }
    if (!seed || !seed.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "seed is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const { suggestions, fromCache } = await getCachedRelatedKeywords(workspaceId, seed.trim(), limit);
    const sorted = [...suggestions].sort((a, b) => (b.searchVolume ?? 0) - (a.searchVolume ?? 0));

    // Best-effort and free -- never let a Trends failure break the response.
    const trending = await fetchTrendingQueries(seed.trim());

    return NextResponse.json({ seed: seed.trim(), suggestions: sorted, fromCache, trending });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 429 });
    }
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("[keywords/explore error]", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { getKeywords } from "@/lib/keywords/repository";
import type { KeywordIntent } from "@/lib/keywords/types";
import type { KeywordSortField, SortDir } from "@/lib/keywords/repository";

const VALID_INTENTS: KeywordIntent[] = ["INFORMATIONAL", "COMMERCIAL", "TRANSACTIONAL", "NAVIGATIONAL"];
const VALID_SORT_FIELDS: KeywordSortField[] = ["text", "position", "volume", "difficulty", "cpc", "impressions", "clicks"];

function parsePositiveInt(value: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const n = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : undefined;
}

function parseNonNegInt(value: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const n = Number.parseInt(value, 10);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? n : undefined;
}

function parseNonNegFloat(value: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!value) return undefined;
  const n = Number.parseFloat(value);
  return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? n : undefined;
}
export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams;
    const workspaceId = sp.get("workspaceId");
    if (!workspaceId) return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const page = parsePositiveInt(sp.get("page")) ?? 1;
    const limit = parsePositiveInt(sp.get("limit")) ?? 25;
    const filter = sp.get("filter") ?? undefined;
    const intentParam = sp.get("intent");
    const intent = intentParam && VALID_INTENTS.includes(intentParam as KeywordIntent) ? (intentParam as KeywordIntent) : undefined;
    const minPosition = parsePositiveInt(sp.get("minPosition"));
    const maxPosition = parsePositiveInt(sp.get("maxPosition"));
    const market = sp.get("market") ?? undefined;
    const language = sp.get("language") ?? undefined;
    const sortByParam = sp.get("sortBy");
    const sortBy = sortByParam && VALID_SORT_FIELDS.includes(sortByParam as KeywordSortField) ? (sortByParam as KeywordSortField) : undefined;
    const sortDirParam = sp.get("sortDir");
    const sortDir: SortDir = sortDirParam === "asc" ? "asc" : "desc";
    const minVolume = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("minVolume"));
    const maxVolume = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("maxVolume"));
    const minKd = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("minKd"));
    const maxKd = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("maxKd"));
    const minCpc = parseNonNegFloat(sp.get("minCpc"));
    const maxCpc = parseNonNegFloat(sp.get("maxCpc"));
    const hasVolume = sp.get("hasVolume") === "1" ? true : undefined;

    const minCompetition = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("minCompetition"));
    const maxCompetition = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("maxCompetition"));
    const minImpressions = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("minImpressions"));
    const maxImpressions = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("maxImpressions"));
    const minClicks = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("minClicks"));
    const maxClicks = parseNonNegInt(sp.get("maxClicks"));
    const minCtr = parseNonNegFloat(sp.get("minCtr"));
    const maxCtr = parseNonNegFloat(sp.get("maxCtr"));
    const minWords = parsePositiveInt(sp.get("minWords"));
    const maxWords = parsePositiveInt(sp.get("maxWords"));
    const include = sp.get("include") ?? undefined;
    const exclude = sp.get("exclude") ?? undefined;
    const exact = sp.get("exact") ?? undefined;
    const hasRanking = sp.get("hasRanking") === "1" ? true : undefined;

    const result = await getKeywords(workspaceId, {
      page,
      limit,
      filter,
      intent,
      minPosition,
      maxPosition,
      market,
      language,
      sortBy,
      sortDir,
      minVolume,
      maxVolume,
      minKd,
      maxKd,
      hasVolume,
      minCpc,
      maxCpc,
      minCompetition,
      maxCompetition,
    });
    return NextResponse.json(result);
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/opportunity-diagnosis/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  authErrorResponse,
  requireUser,
  requireWebsiteAccess,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  getOpportunityDiagnosis,
  OpportunityDiagnosisError,
} from "@/lib/keywords/opportunity-diagnosis";

import {
  buildOpportunityActionPlan,
} from "@/lib/keywords/opportunity-action-plan";

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const websiteId =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get(
          "websiteId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const keyword =
      req.nextUrl.searchParams
        .get(
          "keyword"
        )
        ?.trim();

    const market =
      (
        req.nextUrl.searchParams.get(
          "market"
        ) ??
        "US"
      )
        .trim()
        .toUpperCase();

    if (
      !websiteId ||
      !keyword
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "websiteId and keyword are required.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(
      user.id,
      websiteId
    );

    const website =
      await prisma.website.findUnique({
        where: {
          id:
            websiteId,
        },

        select: {
          url:
            true,
        },
      });

    if (
      !website
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "Website not found.",
        },
        {
          status:
            404,
        }
      );
    }

    const result =
      await getOpportunityDiagnosis({
        siteUrl:
          website.url,

        keyword,

        market,
      });

    const actionPlan =
      buildOpportunityActionPlan(
        result.diagnosis
      );

    return NextResponse.json({
      diagnosis:
        result.diagnosis,

      actionPlan,

      fromCache:
        result.fromCache,
    });
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    if (
      err instanceof
      OpportunityDiagnosisError
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            err.message,
        },
        {
          status:
            err.status,
        }
      );
    }

    console.error(
      "[keywords/opportunity-diagnosis]",
      err instanceof Error
        ? err.message
        : err
    );

    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/sync/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { fetchAndUpsertKeywords } from "@/lib/keywords/ingestion";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { workspaceId, siteUrl } = body as { workspaceId?: string; siteUrl?: string };

    if (!workspaceId || !siteUrl) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId and siteUrl are required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const endDate = new Date();
    const startDate = new Date();
    startDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() - 28);

    const count = await fetchAndUpsertKeywords(workspaceId, siteUrl, {
      startDate,
      endDate,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ success: true, imported: count.keywordsUpserted, snapshotsImported: count.snapshotsUpserted });
  } catch (err) {
    const detail = err && typeof err === "object" && "errors" in err
      ? JSON.stringify((err as { errors?: unknown }).errors)
      : err instanceof Error
        ? err.message
        : String(err);
    console.error("[keywords/sync error]", detail);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/overview/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { normalizeKeyword } from "@/lib/keywords/normalize";
import { fetchSearchVolume, fetchKeywordDifficulty } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import type { Prisma } from "@prisma/client";

const SOURCE = "DATAFORSEO_RELATED";
const VOLUME_SOURCE = "DATAFORSEO_VOLUME";
const CACHE_TTL_DAYS = 30;

const MARKETS = [
  { code: "US", label: "US", flag: "🇺🇸", language: "English", locationName: "United States" },
  { code: "CA", label: "CA", flag: "🇨🇦", language: "English", locationName: "Canada" },
  { code: "GB", label: "GB", flag: "🇬🇧", language: "English", locationName: "United Kingdom" },
  { code: "AU", label: "AU", flag: "🇦🇺", language: "English", locationName: "Australia" },
];

function isFresh(fetchedAt: Date): boolean {
  return Date.now() - fetchedAt.getTime() < CACHE_TTL_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
}

interface MarketMetrics {
  volume: number | null;
  kd: number | null;
  cpc: number | null;
}

async function getMetricsForMarket(keywordText: string, market: string, language: string): Promise<MarketMetrics> {
  const normalized = normalizeKeyword(keywordText);
  const cacheKey = `${normalized}:${market}:${language}`;

  // Check volume cache first
  const cached = await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.findUnique({
    where: { normalized_source: { normalized: cacheKey, source: VOLUME_SOURCE } },
  });
  if (cached && isFresh(cached.fetchedAt)) {
    const p = cached.payload as Record<string, unknown>;
    return {
      volume: typeof p.volume === "number" ? p.volume : null,
      kd: typeof p.kd === "number" ? p.kd : null,
      cpc: typeof p.cpc === "number" ? p.cpc : null,
    };
  }

  // Also check related keywords cache
  const relatedCacheKey = `${normalized}:${market}:${language}`;
  const relatedCached = await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.findUnique({
    where: { normalized_source: { normalized: relatedCacheKey, source: SOURCE } },
  });
  if (relatedCached && isFresh(relatedCached.fetchedAt) && Array.isArray(relatedCached.payload)) {
    const item = (relatedCached.payload as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
      .find((p) => typeof p.keyword === "string" && p.keyword === keywordText);
    if (item) {
      return {
        volume: typeof item.searchVolume === "number" ? item.searchVolume : null,
        kd: typeof item.keywordDifficulty === "number" ? item.keywordDifficulty : null,
        cpc: typeof item.cpc === "number" ? item.cpc : null,
      };
    }
  }

  // Fetch directly from DataForSEO
  const marketObj = MARKETS.find(m => m.code === market);
  if (!marketObj) return { volume: null, kd: null, cpc: null };

  try {
    const [volumeResults, diffResults] = await Promise.all([
      fetchSearchVolume([keywordText], marketObj.locationName, language),
      fetchKeywordDifficulty([keywordText], marketObj.locationName, language),
    ]);
    const vr = volumeResults[0];
    const dr = diffResults[0];
    const metrics: MarketMetrics = {
      volume: vr?.searchVolume ?? null,
      kd: dr?.keywordDifficulty ?? null,
      cpc: vr?.cpc ?? null,
    };
    // Cache the result
    await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.upsert({
      where: { normalized_source: { normalized: cacheKey, source: VOLUME_SOURCE } },
      create: { normalized: cacheKey, source: VOLUME_SOURCE, payload: metrics as unknown as Prisma.InputJsonValue },
      update: { payload: metrics as unknown as Prisma.InputJsonValue, fetchedAt: new Date() },
    });
    return metrics;
  } catch {
    return { volume: null, kd: null, cpc: null };
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const sp = req.nextUrl.searchParams;
    const workspaceId = sp.get("workspaceId");
    const keywordText = sp.get("keyword");
    const market = sp.get("market") ?? "US";
    const language = sp.get("language") ?? "English";

    if (!workspaceId) return NextResponse.json({ error: "workspaceId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    if (!keywordText) return NextResponse.json({ error: "keyword is required." }, { status: 400 });

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    // Get metrics for selected market
    const [selectedMetrics, keyword] = await Promise.all([
      getMetricsForMarket(keywordText, market, language),
      prisma.keyword.findFirst({
        where: { workspaceId, text: keywordText },
        include: { marketMetrics: true },
      }),
    ]);

    // DB overrides cache if available
    const dbMetric = keyword?.marketMetrics.find(m => m.market === market && m.language === language);
    const metrics = {
      volume: dbMetric?.volume ?? selectedMetrics.volume,
      kd: dbMetric?.difficulty ?? selectedMetrics.kd,
      cpc: dbMetric?.cpc ? Number(dbMetric.cpc) : selectedMetrics.cpc,
      competitionIndex: dbMetric?.competitionIndex ?? null,
      intent: keyword?.intent ?? null,
    };

    // Monthly history — from US related cache (most complete)
    let monthlySearches: { year: number; month: number; searchVolume: number }[] = [];
    let trend: { yearly: number | null; quarterly: number | null; monthly: number | null } | null = null;

    const allRelated = await prisma.keywordSuggestionCache.findMany({ where: { source: SOURCE } });
    for (const cached of allRelated) {
      if (!Array.isArray(cached.payload)) continue;
      const item = (cached.payload as Array<Record<string, unknown>>)
        .find((p) => typeof p.keyword === "string" && p.keyword === keywordText);
      if (item) {
        monthlySearches = (item.monthlySearches as typeof monthlySearches) ?? [];
        trend = (item.trend as typeof trend) ?? null;
        break;
      }
    }

    // Global volumes for all markets — parallel
    const globalVolumes = await Promise.all(MARKETS.map(async (m) => {
      const dbM = keyword?.marketMetrics.find(mm => mm.market === m.code && mm.language === m.language);
      if (dbM?.volume) return { code: m.code, label: m.label, flag: m.flag, volume: dbM.volume };
      const cached = await getMetricsForMarket(keywordText, m.code, m.language);
      return { code: m.code, label: m.label, flag: m.flag, volume: cached.volume };
    }));

    return NextResponse.json({
      keyword: keywordText,
      monthlySearches: monthlySearches.sort((a, b) => a.year - b.year || a.month - b.month),
      trend,
      globalVolumes: globalVolumes.filter((g): g is { code: string; label: string; flag: string; volume: number } => g.volume !== null),
      metrics,
    });
  } catch (err) {
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/keywords/analytics/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  authErrorResponse,
  requireUser,
  requireWorkspaceAccess,
} from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

import {
  prisma,
} from "@/lib/prisma";

import {
  aggregateRankSnapshots,
  classifyKeyword,
  dateKey,
  opportunityScore,
  positionDelta,
  shiftUtcDays,
  startOfUtcDay,
  type RankSnapshotInput,
} from "@/lib/keywords/rank-intelligence";

function parseDays(
  value: string | null
): 7 | 28 | 90 {
  if (
    value ===
    "7"
  ) {
    return 7;
  }

  if (
    value ===
    "90"
  ) {
    return 90;
  }

  return 28;
}

function round(
  value: number | null,
  digits = 1
): number | null {
  if (
    value ===
    null
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const factor =
    10 **
    digits;

  return (
    Math.round(
      value *
        factor
    ) /
    factor
  );
}

export async function GET(
  req: NextRequest
) {
  try {
    const user =
      await requireUser();

    const {
      searchParams,
    } =
      new URL(
        req.url
      );

    const workspaceId =
      searchParams
        .get(
          "workspaceId"
        )
        ?.trim();

    if (
      !workspaceId
    ) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        {
          error:
            "workspaceId is required.",
        },
        {
          status:
            400,
        }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(
      user.id,
      workspaceId
    );

    const days =
      parseDays(
        searchParams.get(
          "days"
        )
      );

    const market =
      searchParams.get(
        "market"
      ) ??
      "US";

    const language =
      searchParams.get(
        "language"
      ) ??
      "English";

    const today =
      startOfUtcDay(
        new Date()
      );

    const selectedStart =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -(
          days -
          1
        )
      );

    const historyDays =
      Math.max(
        days *
          2,
        56
      );

    const historyStart =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -(
          historyDays -
          1
        )
      );

    const keywords =
      await prisma.keyword.findMany({
        where: {
          workspaceId,
        },

        select: {
          id:
            true,

          text:
            true,

          volume:
            true,

          difficulty:
            true,

          snapshots: {
            where: {
              source:
                "GOOGLE_SEARCH_CONSOLE",

              date: {
                gte:
                  historyStart,
              },
            },

            orderBy: {
              date:
                "asc",
            },

            select: {
              date:
                true,

              position:
                true,

              impressions:
                true,

              clicks:
                true,

              pageUrl:
                true,
            },
          },

          marketMetrics: {
            where: {
              market,
              language,
            },

            take:
              1,

            select: {
              volume:
                true,

              difficulty:
                true,

              cpc:
                true,

              competitionIndex:
                true,
            },
          },
        },
      });

    const current7Start =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -6
      );

    const previous7Start =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -13
      );

    const previous7End =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -7
      );

    const current28Start =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -27
      );

    const previous28Start =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -55
      );

    const previous28End =
      shiftUtcDays(
        today,
        -28
      );

    const rows =
      keywords.map(
        (
          keyword
        ) => {
          const snapshots:
            RankSnapshotInput[] =
            keyword.snapshots.map(
              (
                snapshot
              ) => ({
                date:
                  snapshot.date,

                position:
                  snapshot.position ===
                  null
                    ? null
                    : Number(
                        snapshot.position
                      ),

                impressions:
                  snapshot.impressions,

                clicks:
                  snapshot.clicks,

                pageUrl:
                  snapshot.pageUrl,
              })
            );

          const selected =
            aggregateRankSnapshots(
              snapshots,
              selectedStart,
              today
            );

          const current7 =
            aggregateRankSnapshots(
              snapshots,
              current7Start,
              today
            );

          const previous7 =
            aggregateRankSnapshots(
              snapshots,
              previous7Start,
              previous7End
            );

          const current28 =
            aggregateRankSnapshots(
              snapshots,
              current28Start,
              today
            );

          const previous28 =
            aggregateRankSnapshots(
              snapshots,
              previous28Start,
              previous28End
            );

          const currentPosition =
            current7.position ??
            selected.position;

          const delta7d =
            positionDelta(
              current7.position,
              previous7.position
            );

          const delta28d =
            positionDelta(
              current28.position,
              previous28.position
            );

          const metric =
            keyword.marketMetrics[0];

          const volume =
            metric?.volume ??
            keyword.volume ??
            null;

          const difficulty =
            metric?.difficulty ??
            keyword.difficulty ??
            null;

          const signal =
            classifyKeyword(
              currentPosition,
              delta7d,
              selected.impressions
            );

          return {
            id:
              keyword.id,

            keyword:
              keyword.text,

            position:
              round(
                currentPosition
              ),

            positionDelta7d:
              round(
                delta7d
              ),

            positionDelta28d:
              round(
                delta28d
              ),

            impressions:
              selected.impressions,

            clicks:
              selected.clicks,

            ctr:
              selected.ctr ===
              null
                ? null
                : round(
                    selected.ctr *
                      100,
                    2
                  ),

            volume,

            difficulty,

            rankingUrl:
              selected.rankingUrl,

            signal,

            opportunityScore:
              opportunityScore(
                currentPosition,
                delta7d,
                selected.impressions,
                volume
              ),
          };
        }
      );

    const selectedSnapshots =
      keywords.flatMap(
        (
          keyword
        ) =>
          keyword.snapshots
            .filter(
              (
                snapshot
              ) =>
                snapshot.date >=
                selectedStart
            )
            .map(
              (
                snapshot
              ) => ({
                keywordId:
                  keyword.id,

                date:
                  snapshot.date,

                position:
                  snapshot.position ===
                  null
                    ? null
                    : Number(
                        snapshot.position
                      ),

                impressions:
                  snapshot.impressions ??
                  0,

                clicks:
                  snapshot.clicks ??
                  0,

                pageUrl:
                  snapshot.pageUrl,
              })
            )
      );

    const overall =
      aggregateRankSnapshots(
        selectedSnapshots.map(
          (
            snapshot
          ) => ({
            date:
              snapshot.date,

            position:
              snapshot.position,

            impressions:
              snapshot.impressions,

            clicks:
              snapshot.clicks,

            pageUrl:
              snapshot.pageUrl,
          })
        ),
        selectedStart,
        today
      );

    const keywordsWithData =
      rows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.position !==
            null ||
          row.impressions >
            0
      ).length;

    const currentRows =
      rows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          row.position !==
          null
      );

    const top3 =
      currentRows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          (
            row.position ??
            Infinity
          ) <=
          3
      ).length;

    const top10 =
      currentRows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          (
            row.position ??
            Infinity
          ) <=
          10
      ).length;

    const top20 =
      currentRows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          (
            row.position ??
            Infinity
          ) <=
          20
      ).length;

    const nearFirstPage =
      currentRows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) => {
          const position =
            row.position ??
            Infinity;

          return (
            position >
              10 &&
            position <=
              20
          );
        }
      ).length;

    const improved =
      rows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          (
            row.positionDelta7d ??
            0
          ) >=
          1
      ).length;

    const declined =
      rows.filter(
        (
          row
        ) =>
          (
            row.positionDelta7d ??
            0
          ) <=
          -1
      ).length;

    const dailyMap =
      new Map<
        string,
        {
          impressions: number;
          clicks: number;
          weightedPosition: number;
          positionWeight: number;
          keywords: Set<string>;
        }
      >();

    for (
      const snapshot of
      selectedSnapshots
    ) {
      const key =
        dateKey(
          snapshot.date
        );

      const bucket =
        dailyMap.get(
          key
        ) ?? {
          impressions:
            0,

          clicks:
            0,

          weightedPosition:
            0,

          positionWeight:
            0,

          keywords:
            new Set<string>(),
        };

      const impressions =
        Math.max(
          0,
          snapshot.impressions
        );

      bucket.impressions +=
        impressions;

      bucket.clicks +=
        Math.max(
          0,
          snapshot.clicks
        );

      if (
        snapshot.position !==
          null &&
        impressions >
          0
      ) {
        bucket.weightedPosition +=
          snapshot.position *
          impressions;

        bucket.positionWeight +=
          impressions;
      }

      if (
        impressions >
        0
      ) {
        bucket.keywords.add(
          snapshot.keywordId
        );
      }

      dailyMap.set(
        key,
        bucket
      );
    }

    const chart:
      Array<{
        date: string;
        impressions: number;
        avgImpressions: number;
        clicks: number;
        avgPosition: number | null;
      }> =
      [];

    for (
      let cursor =
        selectedStart;
      cursor <=
      today;
      cursor =
        shiftUtcDays(
          cursor,
          1
        )
    ) {
      const key =
        dateKey(
          cursor
        );

      const bucket =
        dailyMap.get(
          key
        );

      const keywordCount =
        bucket?.keywords.size ??
        0;

      chart.push({
        date:
          key,

        impressions:
          bucket?.impressions ??
          0,

        avgImpressions:
          keywordCount >
          0
            ? Math.round(
                (
                  (
                    bucket?.impressions ??
                    0
                  ) /
                  keywordCount
                ) *
                  10
              ) /
              10
            : 0,

        clicks:
          bucket?.clicks ??
          0,

        avgPosition:
          bucket &&
          bucket.positionWeight >
            0
            ? round(
                bucket.weightedPosition /
                  bucket.positionWeight
              )
            : null,
      });
    }

    rows.sort(
      (
        a,
        b
      ) =>
        b.opportunityScore -
          a.opportunityScore ||
        b.impressions -
          a.impressions
    );

    return NextResponse.json({
      days,

      scope:
        "workspace",

      stats: {
        trackedKeywords:
          rows.length,

        keywordsWithData,

        impressions:
          overall.impressions,

        avgImpressionsPerKeyword:
          keywordsWithData >
          0
            ? round(
                overall.impressions /
                  keywordsWithData
              )
            : 0,

        clicks:
          overall.clicks,

        ctr:
          overall.ctr ===
          null
            ? null
            : round(
                overall.ctr *
                  100,
                2
              ),

        avgPosition:
          round(
            overall.position
          ),

        top3,
        top10,
        top20,
        nearFirstPage,
        improved,
        declined,
      },

      chart,

      keywords:
        rows.slice(
          0,
          100
        ),
    });
  } catch (
    err
  ) {
    const {
      status,
      body,
    } =
      authErrorResponse(
        err
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      body,
      {
        status,
      }
    );
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/competitors/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import { RateLimitError } from "@/lib/keywords/competitor-cache";
import { listCompetitors } from "@/lib/competitors/repository";
import { analyzeCompetitor } from "@/lib/competitors/service";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const websiteId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("websiteId");
    if (!websiteId) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId is required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const competitors = await listCompetitors(websiteId);
    return NextResponse.json({ competitors });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("competitors GET error", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const body = await req.json();
    const { websiteId, domain, name } = body as { websiteId?: string; domain?: string; name?: string };

    if (!websiteId || !domain || !domain.trim()) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "websiteId and domain are required." }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const website = await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, websiteId);

    const { competitor, snapshot } = await analyzeCompetitor({
      workspaceId: website.business.workspaceId,
      websiteId,
      domain,
      name,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ competitor, snapshot }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 429 });
    }
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("competitors POST error", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/competitors/[id]/refresh/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { requireUser, requireWebsiteAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";
import { DataForSEOError } from "@/lib/keywords/dataforseo-provider";
import { RateLimitError } from "@/lib/keywords/competitor-cache";
import { getCompetitor } from "@/lib/competitors/repository";
import { analyzeCompetitor } from "@/lib/competitors/service";

export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();
    const { id } = await params;

    const competitor = await getCompetitor(id);
    if (!competitor) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: "Competitor not found." }, { status: 404 });
    }

    await requireWebsiteAccess(user.id, competitor.websiteId);

    const { snapshot } = await analyzeCompetitor({
      workspaceId: competitor.website.business.workspaceId,
      websiteId: competitor.websiteId,
      domain: competitor.domain,
      name: competitor.name ?? undefined,
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ snapshot });
  } catch (err) {
    if (err instanceof RateLimitError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 429 });
    }
    if (err instanceof DataForSEOError) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: err.message }, { status: 502 });
    }
    console.error("competitors/refresh POST error", err);
    const { status, body } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(body, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/websites/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";
import { requireUser, requireWorkspaceAccess, authErrorResponse } from "@/lib/auth-helpers";

function normalizeUrl(input: string): string | null {
  try {
    let url = input.trim();
    if (!url.startsWith("http://") && !url.startsWith("https://")) {
      url = "https://" + url;
    }
    const parsed = new URL(url);
    if (!["http:", "https:"].includes(parsed.protocol)) return null;
    return parsed.origin;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const body = await req.json();
    const { businessName, industry, location, websiteUrl, workspaceId } = body as {
      businessName?: string;
      industry?: string;
      location?: string;
      websiteUrl?: string;
      workspaceId?: string;
    };

    if (!businessName || !websiteUrl || !workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Business name, website URL, and workspace are required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const normalizedUrl = normalizeUrl(websiteUrl);
    if (!normalizedUrl) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "Invalid website URL." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const business = await prisma.business.create({
      data: {
        name: businessName,
        industry: industry || null,
        location: location || null,
        workspaceId,
      },
    });

    const website = await prisma.website.create({
      data: {
        url: normalizedUrl,
        businessId: business.id,
      },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ website, business }, { status: 201 });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("websites POST error", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const user = await requireUser();

    const workspaceId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get("workspaceId");
    if (!workspaceId) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: "workspaceId query parameter is required." },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    await requireWorkspaceAccess(user.id, workspaceId);

    const websites = await prisma.website.findMany({
      where: {
        business: {
          workspaceId,
        },
      },
      include: {
        business: true,
        analyses: { orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" }, take: 1 },
      },
      orderBy: { createdAt: "desc" },
    });

    return NextResponse.json({ websites });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("websites GET error", err);
    const { status, body: errBody } = authErrorResponse(err);
    return NextResponse.json(errBody, { status });
  }
}

---------- src/app/api/growth-os/pages/[id]/route.ts ----------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { checkAuth, getPage, patchPage } from "@/lib/cms/mock-provider-store";

export async function GET(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!checkAuth(req.headers.get("authorization"))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid API key" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  const { id } = await params;
  const page = getPage(id);
  if (!page) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return NextResponse.json(page);
}

export async function PATCH(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  if (!checkAuth(req.headers.get("authorization"))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: "Invalid API key" }, { status: 401 });
  }
  const { id } = await params;
  const body = await req.json();
  const updated = patchPage(id, body);
  if (!updated) return NextResponse.json({ error: "Not found" }, { status: 404 });
  return NextResponse.json(updated);
}

17. INTERNAL / ADMIN ROUTE AUTH CHECK
============================================================

---------- src/app/api/internal/outcome-checkpoints/route.ts ----------
import {
  NextRequest,
  NextResponse,
} from "next/server";

import {
  runOutcomeCheckpointEngine,
} from "@/lib/keywords/outcome-checkpoint-engine";

export const runtime =
  "nodejs";

export const dynamic =
  "force-dynamic";

export const maxDuration =
  60;

export async function GET(
  request:
    NextRequest
) {
  const secret =
    process.env.CRON_SECRET
      ?.trim();

  if (
    !secret
  ) {
    console.error(
      "Outcome checkpoint cron is missing CRON_SECRET."
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Scheduler is not configured.",
      },
      {
        status:
          503,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  }

  const authorization =
    request.headers.get(
      "authorization"
    );

  if (
    authorization !==
    `Bearer ${secret}`
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Unauthorized.",
      },
      {
        status:
          401,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  }

  try {
    const result =
      await runOutcomeCheckpointEngine({
        limit:
          100,
      });

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          true,

        result,
      },
      {
        status:
          200,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  } catch (
    error
  ) {
    console.error(
      "Outcome checkpoint cron failed.",
      error
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        ok:
          false,

        error:
          "Checkpoint run failed.",
      },
      {
        status:
          500,

        headers: {
          "Cache-Control":
            "no-store",
        },
      }
    );
  }
}
